Result: 在 24 GiB M4 Pro MacBook 上运行 Qwen3.8-27B MXFP4 的全执行容量测试中，三次独立运行均完成 196,608 输入 + 16,384 输出 token，单请求完成上下文从基线 30,720 提升至 212,992 位置（6.93x）；双请求运行聚合保留 229,376 位置。性能测试：32K 输入/64 输出达 19.11 tokens/s；重复 32K+6K 负载的进程峰值内存中位数为 16,374 MiB。集成运行时在 AIME 2026 上答对 30 题中的 29 题。

Conclusion: 紧凑状态与生命周期感知的执行方式可在有限内存下扩展本地服务容量，并支持更长的生成式推理。

Abstract: Capable open-weight models make local coding and reasoning attractive, but their context and execution state strain laptop memory. We present JustFit, an MLX-based inference runtime that combines KVExec for compressed KV execution, PhaseSwap for component residency, and StateTrans for state-preserving serving transitions. These mechanisms fuse reconstruction and coordinate just-in-time materialization and release, independently of model-weight quantization. In full-execution capacity tests on a 24 GiB M4 Pro MacBook running Qwen3.8-27B MXFP4, three independent runs complete 196,608 input and 16,384 output tokens, increasing completed single-request context from the mlx-vlm baseline's 30,720 positions to 212,992 (6.93x); a separate two-request run retains 229,376 positions in aggregate. In separate performance tests, a 32K-input, 64-output probe reaches 19.11 tokens/s, and a repeated 32K+6K workload has a median peak process footprint of 16,374 MiB. The integrated runtime answers 29 of 30 AIME 2026 problems correctly, showing how compact state and lifetime-aware execution expand local serving capacity while supporting extended generated reasoning.

</details>


### [70] [LimiX-2: A Contextual Mechanism Network Towards General Structured-Data Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17488)
*Xingxuan Zhang,Gang Ren,Hao Yuan,Hao Zou,Hongze Tan,Hui Wang,Jianhao Song,Jiansheng Li,Jiayao Zhang,Jinghan Zhang,Kaifang Li,Lang Mo,Li Mao,Mingchao Hao,Nuo Xu,Rui Ding,Ruiji Zhang,Shuyang Li,Siyu Mei,Tianyang Zhang,Weiyang Mu,Yancheng Dong,Yongxian Wei,Yuan Xue,Yuanrui Wang,Yue He,Zijia Yang,Ziyun Li,Dongzhe Li,Fuqiang Wang,Jiandong Liu,Jiawei Chen,Jiaxin Du,Kaijie Cheng,Kehan Li,Lei Sun,Linjun Zhou,Ningbo Dai,Qi Wang,Renzhe Xu,Shaoxing Du,Shumeng Yang,Wang Lu,Wenjing Chu,Xiannan Huang,Xiaoyu Lin,Xing Ai,Xinyan Han,Xuanyue Li,Xuanyue Su,Xukun Zhang,Yan Lu,Yaxin Zhang,Yi Qin,Yifei Huang,Yihan Xu,Yongle Lv,Yuanyuan Jiang,Yushan Han,Peng Cui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LimiX-2 是 LimiX 家族的新模型，基于既有缩放规律进行模型与数据扩展，采用上下文机制网络（CMNs）范式与上下文条件掩码建模（CCMM）预训练，将表格 PFN 的组织原则从目标中心预测转向机制导向的联合建模，在多个表格基准上超越现有专用模型与表格基础模型，并展现出因果感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统表格 PFN 以预测目标 p(y | x, D_context) 为中心，这种目标中心的范式限制了模型对数据生成机制的理解。作者希望借助缩放规律扩展模型与数据规模，并通过范式转变让模型学习数据背后的联合结构与生成机制，从而同时提升预测性能与因果感知能力。

Method: 提出 LimiX-2，采用上下文机制网络（CMNs）范式，将组织原则由目标中心预测改为机制导向的联合建模，围绕 p(x, y | D_context) 学习依赖上下文的联合结构表示；预训练使用上下文条件掩码建模（CCMM），训练数据为结构因果模型（SCMs）生成的合成数据集，覆盖多样的图结构、功能机制与观测过程。

Result: 在 TabArena、TALENT 和 BCCO 上的评测显示，LimiX-2 优于当前的数据集专用模型和表格基础模型。除预测性能外，CMN 范式还带来因果感知能力：其特征注意力编码了直接因果关系，能够实现准确的因果骨架恢复。

Conclusion: LimiX-2 验证了以缩放规律指导的模型与数据扩展结合 CMNs 范式的有效性，说明从目标中心预测转向机制导向联合建模不仅提升表格预测表现，还能赋予模型因果结构发现能力，为表格基础模型提供了新的建模范式方向。

Abstract: We introduce LimiX-2, a new model in the LimiX family, developed through model and data scaling guided by our previously established scaling laws. LimiX-2 adopts the Contextual Mechanism Networks (CMNs) paradigm and is pretrained with Context-Conditional Masked Modeling (CCMM). CMNs shifts the organizing principle of in-context learning from target-centric prediction to mechanism-oriented joint modeling. Rather than centering the network on the $p(y \mid x, D_{\mathrm{context}})$ objective of conventional tabular PFNs, it is designed around learning $p(x, y \mid D_{\mathrm{context}})$, a context-dependent representation of the joint structure underlying data generation. Pretraining uses synthetic datasets generated by structural causal models (SCMs) spanning diverse graph structures, functional mechanisms, and observation processes. Evaluations on TabArena, TALENT, and BCCO show that LimiX-2 outperforms current dataset-specific models and tabular foundation models. Beyond predictive performance, the CMN paradigm also promotes causal awareness in LimiX-2: its feature attention encodes direct causal relationships, enabling accurate causal skeleton recovery.

</details>


### [71] [Verifiable Social Reasoning for LLM Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17496)
*Amir Taubenfeld,Zorik Gekhman,Avigail Grinstein-Dabush,Itay Laish,Ariel Goldstein,Marian Croak,Avinatan Hassidim,Yossi Matias,Amir Feder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Fuse——一个多智能体仿真框架，用于在用户中介的咨询场景中评估 LLM 的社会推理能力，并通过 12 个 LLM 的实验揭示用户中介、用户偏差框架等因素对社会推理的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 助手广泛用于日常社交建议，但在咨询场景中评估其社会推理存在两大挑战：(i) 需要让助手从主观的用户叙述中了解社交情境；(ii) 社会属性（如他人意图）通常缺乏可验证的真实标签。因此亟需一种可构造可验证真值、且贴近真实咨询场景的评估方法。

Method: 提出 Fuse 多智能体仿真框架：一个带有隐藏动机的目标智能体与其他智能体（含代表用户的智能体）交互，用户随后咨询被评估的助手以推断目标动机，由此在构建层面提供可验证真值。通过 2.4 万条标注的人类研究验证仿真的真实性，并在 12 个 LLM 上系统应用，隔离关键因素进行分析。

Result: (i) 用户中介加剧了社会推理本身的难度；(ii) LLM 对带偏见的用户框架表现出系统性敏感性；(iii) 模型可能需要比人类更多的细节才能做出正确预测；(iv) 更长的对话并不总能提升表现，尽管提供了澄清提问的机会。开源 Fuse 及含 2.1 万条样本的数据集。

Conclusion: Fuse 为研究用户中介的社会推理提供了可验证真值的仿真评估方案，系统性揭示了 LLM 在社交咨询中的若干局限，并开源框架与数据集以支持后续研究。

Abstract: LLM assistants are widely used for daily social advice, yet evaluating their social reasoning in such consultation settings remains challenging since (i) it requires setups where the assistant learns about social situations from subjective user narratives, and (ii) social properties, such as others' intentions, typically lack verifiable ground truth. To address these challenges, we introduce Fuse, a multi-agent simulation framework for studying user-mediated social reasoning. In Fuse, a target agent with a hidden motive interacts with other agents including one representing the user, who then consults the evaluated assistant to infer the target's motive, providing verifiable ground truth by construction. Simulation faithfulness is validated through a human study with 24k annotations. We apply Fuse to 12 LLMs and demonstrate its analytical utility by systematically isolating key factors, showing that (i) user mediation compounds the inherent difficulty of social reasoning; (ii) LLMs exhibit systematic sensitivity to biased user framing; (iii) models can require more details than humans need to reach a correct prediction; and (iv) longer conversations do not always improve performance despite providing opportunities for clarifying questions. We open-source Fuse and a dataset with 21k examples.

</details>


### [72] [ScienceBuddy: Recursive-in-Recursive Self-Improvement for Interactive Scientific Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17523)
*Shuhan Xue,Jianyuan Zhong,Ziyuan Nan,Wenbin Li,Zhaochen Yu,Jinchao Ding,Qiang Gao,Pengyu Zhan,Yuntong Zhang,Tian Cheng,Zhenfei Yin,Yingcheng Wu,Ling Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍并发布 ScienceBuddy——一个交互式科研工作空间，将持续改进的科学智能体融入研究者日常工作流。其核心为"递归中的递归自我改进"范式，通过 harness 演化（内层，模型固定）与模型强化学习（外层，固定改进后的 harness）交替耦合，实现系统与科研共同进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者日常科研工作与日益改进的科学智能体之间存在割裂，缺乏一个能让智能体在真实科研交互中持续学习、并与研究者协同进化的平台。作者希望将研究者的请求、反馈与执行证据转化为可训练任务和评估标准，从而推动"发现智能"——与研究协作共进的科学 AI。

Method: 提出 ScienceBuddy 交互式科研工作空间，核心是 recursive-in-recursive self-improvement 范式：内层递归在模型固定时改进 harness，外层递归在改进后的 harness 下训练模型；harness 演化塑造训练经验，模型学习又为 harness 适配创造新机会。同时将研究者交互行为转化为任务与评估 rubric，用于持续学习。通过覆盖四类科学任务族的基准案例进行验证。

Result: 展示了三类案例研究：研究者交互、harness 精炼与模型学习；基准案例覆盖四个科学任务族，验证了该范式在真实科研工作流中的可行性。

Conclusion: 通过将 ScienceBuddy 作为研究产品发布，作者使该范式可供科学社区使用，向"发现智能"迈进一步：即通过与研究者持续协作而进步、并与其所支持的科研共同演化的科学 AI。

Abstract: We introduce and release ScienceBuddy, an interactive scientific research workspace that brings continually improving scientific agents into researchers' everyday workflows. ScienceBuddy supports researchers in carrying out scientific tasks while transforming their requests, feedback, and execution evidence into tasks and evaluation rubrics for continual learning. At its core is recursive-in-recursive self-improvement, a paradigm that couples harness evolution with model reinforcement learning: the inner recursion improves the harness with the model fixed, while the outer recursion trains the model under the improved harness. Harness evolution shapes training experience, and model learning creates new opportunities for harness adaptation. We present case studies of researcher interaction, harness refinement, and model learning, with the benchmark cases spanning four scientific task families. By releasing ScienceBuddy as a research product, we make this paradigm available to the scientific community and take a step toward discovery intelligence: scientific AI that advances through sustained collaboration with researchers and evolves alongside the research it supports. Website: http://science-buddy.io

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [73] [Evaluating Brand Retrieval and Ranking in Large Language Model Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16304)
*Edward Malthouse,Kun-Yu Lee,Jing Yang,Sanchary Pal,Xueyan Feng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出一套评估开放式LLM品牌推荐的框架，将竞争集独立于模型输出定义，并通过重复采样量化推荐概率与显著性，识别出LLM推荐是随机检索-排序过程而非单一列表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益用于产品推荐，但其评估不同于传统信息检索与推荐系统：LLM可在无显式候选集下生成推荐，且对同一查询重复响应会产生不同品牌与排序。因此需要新的评估范式和指标来刻画这种开放式、随机化的推荐行为。

Method: 提出开放式LLM品牌推荐评估框架：独立于模型输出定义竞争集（competition set），通过重复采样估计推荐概率与显著性。操作化为两个指标——品牌推荐概率BRP@k与平均倒数排名MRR@k。在5个产品类别、6个LLM上应用该框架，并设计三类查询：仅类别查询、需求导向查询、诊断性定位探针。

Result: 仅类别查询显示：知名品牌大量缺失，且推荐显著性并未遵循传统品牌流行度；显著性更多与更广泛的市场可见性信号相关，尤其是搜索兴趣和线上品牌讨论。需求导向查询表明，加入用户目标与约束会改变被检索品牌；诊断性定位探针表明，普通推荐中被遗漏的品牌在提供独特线索时仍可被条件性检索到。

Conclusion: 应将LLM推荐评估为随机的检索-排序过程，而非依据单次生成的列表。作者开源了软件与数据，以支持LLM生成品牌推荐的可复现评估。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for product recommendation, but evaluating their recommendations presents challenges that differ from conventional information retrieval and recommender systems. LLMs can generate recommendations without an explicit candidate set, and repeated responses to the same query can produce different brands and rankings. We introduce a framework for evaluating open-ended LLM brand recommendations that defines the competitive set independently of model outputs and estimates recommendation prevalence and prominence through repeated sampling. We operationalize these constructs using Brand Recommendation Probability (BRP@$k$) and Mean Reciprocal Rank (MRR@$k$), and apply the framework to six LLMs across five product categories. Category-only queries reveal substantial omission of established brands and limited evidence that recommendation prominence follows conventional brand popularity. Instead, prominence is associated with broader marketplace-visibility signals, particularly search interest and online brand conversation. Needs-based queries show that contextualizing users' goals and constraints changes which brands are retrieved, while diagnostic positioning probes demonstrate that brands omitted from ordinary recommendations can remain conditionally retrievable when distinctive cues are supplied. These findings highlight the need to evaluate LLM recommendation as a stochastic retrieval-and-ranking process rather than from individual generated lists. We provide open-source software and data to support reproducible evaluation of LLM-generated brand recommendations.

</details>


### [74] [Where Post-Training Quantization Breaks Text Embedders: A Measured Map Across Four Embedder Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16391)
*Hyojung Han*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该工作系统检验了从LLM量化迁移到检索嵌入器的三条经验法则，发现它们在检索场景下均不成立：嵌入表并非主导保护对象，模块敏感度排序不可迁移，重建误差代理具有误导性；蒸馏小模型在特定任务域内可超越其教师模型的极端量化版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权重-only的训练后量化(PTQ)被视为压缩检索嵌入器成本最低的方案，而社区长期沿用三条源自实践的经验建议：保护嵌入表、按模块敏感度分配比特、使用排序感知目标而非权重重构。作者希望验证这些在LLM量化中"原封不动"移植过来的建议是否对检索嵌入器真正有效。

Method: 对来自4个架构家族的5个检查点进行权重only的PTQ，在比特宽度与group size构成的网格上遍历搜索，并在每个位宽下分别隔离嵌入块、注意力块与前馈块进行消融，以检验保护优先级、模块敏感度排序及重建代理指标的有效性。

Result: 三条启发式均未按原样迁移。嵌入表在任何家族中都不是主导的隔离保护优先项，尽管它在若干架构中是最大张量。模块敏感度不构成可迁移排序：INT4/g16下模块差异过小不足以分配比特；INT3下排序变为家族依赖，且联合损伤不再是各部分之和；INT2下相同重建误差对应的保真度（full precision保留比例）横跨1.3%至65.9%。廉价重建代理适合筛选均匀位宽，但对选择保护张量可靠性显著下降，其跨网格的"表面强度"实为取值范围扩展(range-extension)造成的假象。

Conclusion: 在检索嵌入器上，LLM量化的常规启发式不能直接套用；极端PTQ下检索质量高度依赖家族与位宽配置。一个109M蒸馏学生模型在INT3下以68.4MB取得78.04 NDCG@10，在体积与质量上同时优于其0.6B教师(297.9MB, 64.46)，但仅在其蒸馏所针对的任务域内成立。报告中的体积均为实际文件字节数而非算术估算，且测量仓库提供了每个数值的字节来源可追溯信息。

Abstract: Weight-only post-training quantization is the cheapest way to shrink a retrieval embedder, and the received advice for applying it -- protect the embedding table, allocate bits by module sensitivity, prefer a ranking-aware objective over weight reconstruction -- was carried into LLM quantization largely intact. We test that advice on retrieval embedders directly, quantizing five checkpoints from four architecture families across a grid of bit widths and group sizes, and isolating the embedding, attention and feed-forward blocks at each width.
  Every heuristic fails to transfer as stated. The embedding table never emerges as the dominant isolated protection priority in any family, despite being the largest tensor in several of them. Module sensitivity does not survive as a transferable ordering: at INT4/g16 the spread between modules is too small to allocate against, at INT3 the ordering becomes family-dependent and joint damage stops being the sum of its parts, and at INT2 comparable reconstruction error accompanies retention ranging from 1.3 to 65.9 percent of full precision. A cheap reconstruction proxy is useful for screening uniform bit widths but substantially less reliable for choosing which tensors to protect; its apparent strength across the whole grid is a range-extension artifact.
  A distilled 109M student at INT3 holds 78.04 NDCG@10 in 68.4 MB and dominates the extreme-PTQ arm of its own 0.6B teacher, 297.9 MB at 64.46, on both size and quality -- but only inside the task it was distilled for. Sizes are byte counts of files that exist rather than arithmetic estimates, and the measurement repository carries the byte provenance for every one of them.

</details>


### [75] [Balancing Trial and Reorder: A Hybrid Sequential Transformer-GBDT Ranker for On-Demand Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16407)
*Marcel Kurovski,Attila Nagy,Steffen Klempau,Aleksandr Fedintsev*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 Universal Venue Ranker (UVR)，一套部署于 Wolt 外卖平台的统一门店排序系统：以双向 Transformer 编码器建模用户行为序列，结合融合上下文、用户与门店特征的 GBDT 排序器，在推理阶段施加本地配送约束，用单模型替代原有的四个排序模型（三个餐饮、一个零售）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外卖平台上门店排序直接决定用户可发现与下单的对象；与纯数字域不同，候选门店具有本地性，并受实时可用性与配送运营约束。核心建模矛盾在于：既要为新店曝光以促进尝试，又要为具有复购意图的会话保持排序质量。

Method: UVR 由双向 Transformer 编码器（序列用户建模）与 GBDT 排序器（整合上下文、用户、门店特征）组成；训练覆盖一个国家内全部门店与业务域，推理时强制本地配送约束。通过标签平滑与偏向尝试（trial-biased）的样本加权引导模型倾向新店，从而在单一系统中平衡尝试与复购两类意图。

Result: 离线实验中，尝试类 MRR 相对生产模型提升 +12% 至 +30%，但六个国家中有五个的复购 MRR 出现下降；混合两类会话的核心在线指标 Global CVR 统计上无显著变化。三次连续 A/B 测试（前两次覆盖 Wolt 最大运营市场，第三次覆盖全部运营国家与两个业务域）显示：V1 相比原生产排序器带来 +5.5% Merchant Trial Rate 与 +0.16% Global CVR；V2 在此基础上再增 +0.45% Merchant Trial Rate；V3（餐饮与零售排序器的跨域统一）再增 +1.31% Retail Merchant Trial Rate。

Conclusion: UVR 以单一统一系统替代四个既有排序模型，在保持核心在线指标不降的前提下显著提升商家尝试率，并带来可观的增量订单总额与大幅简化的服务栈，验证了统一跨域排序与试错—复购权衡策略在生产环境中的可行性。

Abstract: On a delivery platform, personalized store ranking greatly influences what users find and order. Unlike digital-only domains, candidate stores are local and bound by real-time availability and delivery operations. One central modeling tension is between surfacing new stores for trial and preserving ranking quality for sessions with reorder intent. We present Universal Venue Ranker (UVR), a production system deployed at Wolt that pairs a bidirectional transformer encoder for sequential user modeling with a GBDT ranker integrating contextual, user, and store features. Trained across all stores and domains of a country while enforcing local delivery constraints at inference, UVR replaces four previously separate ranking models (three for restaurants, one for retail) with a single unified system. Label smoothing and trial-biased sample weighting steer the model toward new stores, lifting offline trial MRR by +12% to +30% over production while regressing reorder MRR in five of six countries. These regressions leave Global CVR, our core online metric, which blends trial and reorder sessions, statistically unchanged. We validate UVR in three consecutive A/B tests, the first two across Wolt's largest operating markets and the third spanning all operating countries and both domains. UVR V1 delivers +5.5% Merchant Trial Rate and +0.16% Global CVR over the previous production ranker; V2 adds a further +0.45% Merchant Trial Rate on top; and V3, our cross-domain unification of the restaurant and retail rankers, adds a further +1.31% Retail Merchant Trial Rate, together accounting for substantial incremental gross order value and a materially simplified serving stack.

</details>


### [76] [PCap: Personalized Retrieval-Stage Diversity Capping in Facebook Marketplace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16452)
*Guangchao Yuan,Janis Fuh,Christopher Choate,Xun Tang,Wenqi Zhu,Chengyi Zhang,Pavan Kumar Paalya Chandrashekar,Jiang Han,Jiangyuan Li,Hongyan Wang,Shuting Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出个性化类目上限框架PCap，在检索阶段引入用户级多样性约束，以提升Facebook Marketplace的多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业检索系统通常侧重相关性而忽略多样性，且用户对多样性的偏好存在个体差异，需要在检索阶段引入个性化多样性约束。

Method: PCap基于Shannon熵对用户多样性偏好打分，将用户分入不同多样性分桶，在多源候选检索中施加个性化类目上限；并采用自动化在线优化方法Parameter Tuning Sequence搜索每个分桶上限的高维参数空间。

Result: 大规模在线实验表明，PCap在互动指标上显著改善了用户的浏览体验。

Conclusion: 该工作为在工业检索系统中集成个性化多样性提供了可落地的实践参考。

Abstract: We propose a personalized capping framework (PCap) to improve the diversity in Facebook Marketplace by introducing user-level diversity constraints at the retrieval stage. PCap models individual diversity preferences using Shannon entropy-based scoring, segments users into diversity buckets, and applies personalized category caps during multi-source candidate retrieval. To navigate the high-dimensional parameter space of per-bucket caps, we leverage an automated online optimization method called Parameter Tuning Sequence. Large-scale online experiments demonstrate that PCap significantly improves users' browsing experience shown in engagement metrics. This work provides practical insights into integrating personalized diversity into industrial retrieval systems.

</details>


### [77] [Predicting Partial Answer Quality and Utility in Agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16453)
*Fangzheng Tian,Debasis Ganguly,Craig Macdonald*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出轨迹内探测框架，通过每轮检索-推理迭代后强制生成中间答案，定义部分答案质量与部分效用两个迭代级指标，并研究其可预测性及用于早停的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agentic RAG评估主要关注端到端结果，缺乏对生成过程中答案状态如何变化的可见性，无法了解各迭代轮次的边际贡献。

Method: 在每次检索-推理迭代后强制模型停止推理并生成中间答案，定义部分答案质量（各迭代答案质量）与部分效用（跨迭代质量变化）；基于轨迹信号（迭代内、迭代间、查询-迭代视角）构建部分答案质量预测与部分效用预测任务。

Result: 多跳QA基准上，部分答案质量常在自然终止前趋于平台期，后续迭代仅带来微小提升；部分答案质量比部分效用更易预测，监督模型质量预测Pearson r超过0.43；利用预测的质量与效用进行早停，平均迭代次数减少约11%，同时保留约98%的自然停止所达最终答案质量。

Conclusion: 轨迹内探测可有效刻画Agentic RAG中间答案状态，部分答案质量具有较好可预测性，据此实现早停能在几乎不损失最终质量的前提下显著降低计算开销。

Abstract: Agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has become a promising paradigm for multi-hop question answering, where a reasoning model iteratively issues queries to a retriever and incorporates newly retrieved context into subsequent reasoning steps. While this iterative process can improve final answer quality, current evaluations of agentic RAG largely focus on end-to-end outcomes and provide limited visibility into how a model's answer state changes during generation. In this work, we introduce an in-trajectory probing framework to study intermediate answer states in agentic RAG. Specifically, after each retrieval-reasoning iteration, we force an agentic model to stop reasoning and generate an intermediate answer based on its current state. This allows us to define two iteration-level measures: partial answer quality at each iteration, and partial utility as the change in partial answer quality across iterations. Our analysis across multi-hop QA benchmarks reveals that partial answer quality often plateaus before natural termination, with many later iterations contributing only small measurable improvements. Accordingly, we formulate two prediction tasks, partial answer quality prediction and partial utility prediction, and study trajectory-derived signals from intra-iteration, inter-iteration, and query-iteration perspectives. Experiments show that partial answer quality is more predictable than partial utility, with supervised models achieving Pearson's r above 0.43 for quality prediction. Finally, using predicted answer quality and utility for early stopping reduces average iteration count by about 11% while preserving about 98% of the final answer quality achieved by natural stopping.

</details>


### [78] [ReliGRec: Reliability-Oriented LLM-Based Generative Recommendation via User-Risk-Aware Prompt Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16560)
*Haoran Yang,Fei Chen,Yutian Xiao,Jiahao Liang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出ReliGRec，一个弱监督的可靠性导向生成式推荐框架，将用户级弱风险估计从辅助预测转化为生成时的控制信号，通过双视图弱风险估计器在推理时选择简单或谨慎提示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实推荐系统中用户行为异质：部分用户偏好连贯，另一些则出现兴趣突变、爆发式交互、过度重复或与协同邻域不一致。这些偏差可能来自良性差异，也可能来自刷单等操纵行为，但单独不足以确立恶意意图。现有鲁棒推荐方法通过训练时重加权或图聚合利用用户风险信号，而在基于LLM的生成式推荐中，根据估计的用户级弱风险来调整生成过程仍研究不足。

Method: ReliGRec从评论反馈信号中为部分用户推导用户级弱风险代理标签。它分别用Behavior Token表示序列行为、用时序Graph Tokens表示协同上下文，再由双视图弱风险估计器融合两者表示，产生用户级弱风险分数，在推理时选择简单提示或谨慎提示。谨慎提示鼓励关注稳定、协同支持的证据，减少对孤立、短期或重复交互的过度依赖。Behavior Token通过弱风险估计与路由影响生成，聚合的Graph Token为下一项的语义ID生成提供协同上下文。

Result: 实验报告了具有竞争力的推荐性能和弱风险代理标签预测效果，同时路由分析刻画了弱风险引导提示在推荐质量与推理成本方面的行为特征。

Conclusion: ReliGRec将弱风险估计从辅助预测转变为生成时的控制信号，为LLM生成式推荐中按用户级弱风险自适应生成提供了可行路径。

Abstract: User behavior in real-world recommender systems is heterogeneous. While some users exhibit coherent preferences, others show abrupt interest shifts, bursty interactions, excessive repetition, or inconsistency with collaborative neighborhoods. Such deviations may arise from benign variation or manipulation, including shilling attacks, but do not alone establish malicious intent. Existing robust recommenders exploit user-risk signals through training-time reweighting or graph aggregation, whereas adapting generation to estimated user-level weak risk remains underexplored in LLM-based generative recommendation. We propose ReliGRec (Reliability-oriented Generative Recommendation), a weakly supervised framework whose name denotes its design goal rather than a supervised reliability variable. ReliGRec derives user-level weak-risk proxy labels from review-feedback signals for a subset of users and represents sequential behavior and collaborative context using a Behavior Token and temporal Graph Tokens, respectively. A Dual-View Weak-Risk Estimator fuses the representations to produce a user-level weak-risk score that selects a Simple or Cautious Prompt at inference. The Cautious Prompt is designed to encourage attention to stable, collaboratively supported evidence while reducing overreliance on isolated, short-term, or repeated interactions. The Behavior Token affects generation through weak-risk estimation and routing, whereas the aggregated Graph Token provides collaborative context for next-item Semantic ID generation. ReliGRec thus turns weak-risk estimation from an auxiliary prediction into a generation-time control signal. Experiments report competitive recommendation and weak-risk proxy-label prediction, while routing analyses characterize the recommendation-quality and inference-cost behavior of weak-risk-guided prompting.

</details>


### [79] [Measuring Decision-Scale Use in Tool-Augmented LLMs: A Contrastive Urban Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16607)
*Ray Chen,Vivian Wong,Christan Grant*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 URBANCONTRASTIVEQA 基准，考察工具增强语言模型能否基于地点历史基线做"相对异常性"比较，而非简单比较原始活动量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市决策支持的核心问题往往是"某地活动是否异常偏高或偏低"，而非"哪个地方原始计数更大"。例如安静社区的 20 次接单可能比机场的 180 次更异常。然而现有工具增强模型通常只接触到原始活动量，缺乏对局部基线的表达，导致其误判异常性。

Method: 构建 URBANCONTRASTIVEQA 基准：每个样本配对两个来自纽约、芝加哥、西雅图公开出行数据的城市情境，并按当前活动偏离该地历史基线的程度打标签。评测六种指令微调模型在五种工具输出格式下的表现，包括仅原始计数、服务器计算的基线分数以及序数标签等。

Result: 仅提供原始计数时，模型常选择数值更大的地点，即使该数值对其所在区域并不异常；提供服务器计算的基线分数和序数标签后，准确率提升，但提升幅度因模型而异。

Conclusion: 面向异构城市数据流，工具接口不应只暴露活动量，还需暴露局部基线信息。论文公开了配对库、标签、评分脚本和数据卡片。

Abstract: Urban decision-support often asks whether activity is unusually high or low for a specific place, not which place has the larger raw count. Twenty pickups in a quiet neighborhood can be more abnormal than 180 at an airport. We introduce URBANCONTRASTIVEQA, a benchmark that asks whether tool-augmented language models can make this baseline-relative comparison. Each item pairs two urban situations from public mobility data in NYC, Chicago, and Seattle, labeled by how far current activity deviates from that place's historical baseline. We evaluate six instruction-tuned models under five tool-output formats. With only raw counts, models often pick the larger number even when it is less abnormal for its zone. Server-computed baseline scores and ordinal labels raise accuracy, but gains vary by model. For heterogeneous urban feeds, tool interfaces need to expose local baselines, not just activity volumes. We release the pair bank, labels, scoring scripts, and data card.

</details>


### [80] [AURA: Agentic Diagnosis and Refinement for Production Recommender Systems at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16625)
*SungGeun Kim,Abhinav Narain,Daniel Nemirovsky*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出AURA，一个端到端智能体系统，通过专用智能体对生产推荐日志进行大规模定性评估，定位推荐失败模式，并基于代码库上下文自动生成代码级算法改进，在两家大型消费者平台的初始测试中验证了可行性与可迁移架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合量化指标只能提供高层且不完整的图景，难以辨析推荐在何处、为何对终端用户表现不佳；改进算法往往依赖利益相关方反馈、领域经验与数据分析的混合，缺乏可扩展的客观性与领域理解。因此需要一个能在规模上对推荐质量进行定性诊断并直接落地改进的系统。

Method: AURA采用智能体架构：专用智能体读取从数千到数百万会话的生产互动日志，规模化执行定性评估，提炼推荐失败的真实用户模式与案例；随后结合推荐系统自身代码、数据与训练流水线的上下文，提出并实现扎根于该代码库的算法细化。系统设计强调配置层隔离领域特定元素以支持跨平台迁移，并配套安全保障与运维经验。

Result: 在一家主要媒体流媒体公司的两个大型消费者平台的生产数据上完成初始测试，报告了系统设计、安全保障、运维经验及初步结果，验证了向自我改进推荐系统演进的方向。

Conclusion: AURA所弥合的诊断缺口并不限于流媒体场景；其架构具备可迁移性，所有领域特定要素均通过配置层注入，并已在两个平台间完成移植。论文进一步将该方法具体映射到电子商务与在线零售推荐场景。

Abstract: How and why does a recommender system fail the users it serves? Oftentimes, practitioners are left to improve their algorithms based on a combination of feedback from stakeholder teams, domain expertise, and insights from data analyses. Yet the nuances of how and where recommendations perform well or poorly for end users are difficult to discern from aggregate quantitative metrics. Whereas these metrics provide a high-level and incomplete picture, further granularity into the quality of recommendations and their patterns requires reasoning with domain understanding and objectivity, at scale. We contemplate this complex conundrum and describe a method and implementation that uses the latest AI agentic advances to provide actionable diagnoses and improvements for production recommender systems. We present AURA (Agentic Understanding and Refinement of recommender Algorithms), an end-to-end agentic system that performs qualitative evaluation at scale and can then generate improvements to our algorithms at the code level. Specialized agents read production engagement logs, from thousands of sessions to millions, and surface patterns and examples of how the recommender fails real users. The next step uses those diagnoses and context about the recommender's own code, data, and training pipeline to propose and implement refinements grounded in that codebase. We report the system design, initial tests on production data from two large consumer platforms at a major media-streaming company, safeguards, operational learnings, and early results toward a self-improving recommender system. Finally, the diagnostic gap AURA closes is not specific to streaming. The architecture is built to transfer: every domain-specific element enters through the configuration layer that already ported it between our two platforms. We map it concretely to e-commerce and online-retail recommendation.

</details>


### [81] [Efficient Swing Computation for Retrieval in Large-Scale Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16850)
*Runhao Jiang,Renchi Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出ASC与K-ASC两种高效算法，用于近似及Top-K Swing查询，在保持查询质量的同时实现数量级加速，并能处理十亿级交互图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Swing分数在i2i检索与工业级推荐系统中效果显著，但现有计算方法存在两方面缺陷：一是关于物品度数呈二次时间复杂度，代价过高；二是依赖截断启发式，质量不理想，难以适用于十亿级交互规模的大图。

Method: 提出ASC与K-ASC两种算法。ASC通过非平凡的简洁方式组合GNS与USS两种随机化算法，对高度数与低度数查询物品自适应处理，以最小运行时开销提供Swing值在概率相对误差与加性误差上的严格理论保证；K-ASC采用过滤-精炼（filter-refinement）范式并辅以精心设计的启发式策略，面向Top-K查询兼顾效率与效果。

Result: 在八个真实数据集上的大量实验表明，ASC与K-ASC在计算时间上相较对比方法可实现数量级加速，同时保持相同的近似与Top-K查询结果质量；K-ASC在包含十亿边规模的Yambda与MAG等大规模图上表现尤为高效。

Conclusion: ASC与K-ASC有效克服了现有Swing分数计算在复杂度与质量上的局限，具备严格理论保证与良好实用性，可用于大规模推荐系统中的近似与Top-K Swing查询。

Abstract: Given a user-item graph $G$, a query item $v_q$ and a target item $v_t$, the Swing score $sw(v_q, v_t)$ of the item pair $(v_q, v_t)$ leverages the user-item-user interaction structure to evaluate their similarity. This measure is found to be highly effective in item-to-item (i2i) retrieval task and finds extensive applications in industrial-scale recommender systems. However, existing solutions towards computing Swing scores are either prohibitively expensive due to their quadratic time complexity w.r.t. the item degree, or rely on truncation heuristics that yield unsatisfactory quality, rendering them impractical particularly on graphs with billions of interactions.
  In this paper, we present ASC and $K$-ASC, two novel and efficient algorithms for approximate and top-$K$ Swing queries, to address the aforementioned limitations. Specifically, these algorithms provide rigorous theoretical guarantees in probabilistic relative and additive errors of Swing values. The basic idea of ASC is to combine two randomized algorithms, GNS and USS, in a simple yet non-trivial way to adaptively process high- and low-degree query items with minimal runtime cost. In particular, $K$-ASC offers practical efficiency and effectiveness for top-$K$ queries through a filter-refinement paradigm with carefully-designed heuristics. Extensive experiments over eight real datasets demonstrate that ASC and $K$-ASC can achieve orders of magnitude speed-up over competitors in terms of computational time while offering the same approximate and top-$K$ query result quality, and in particular, $K$-ASC is highly efficient on massive graphs including the billion-edge Yambda and MAG datasets.

</details>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [82] [Causal neural set filtering for online multi-target tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16054)
*Zhongdi Liu,Huangyu Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出因果神经集合滤波（CNSF），仅编码当前量测、以递归航迹状态携带历史证据，在保持关联与状态估计性能的同时大幅降低计算量与参数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Transformer 类多目标跟踪器（MT3/Track-MT3）反复重编码量测窗口，导致冗余计算，效率低且参数量大。

Method: CNSF 采用递归结构：仅编码当前量测，历史证据保存在结构化递归航迹状态中；结合排他性 Sinkhorn 关联、关联条件化的卡尔曼式更新与矩匹配、以及递归伯努利生命周期建模与量测驱动的新生机制。

Result: 在三类工况的仿真留出测试集上，相比 Track-MT3，平均 GOSPA 与 T-GOSPA 分别降低 19.3% 和 30.4%，参数量减少 55.9%，单线程 CPU 推理加速 3.76 倍。

Conclusion: CNSF 通过因果递归集合滤波替代窗口重编码范式，在软一对一约束、关联不确定性传播及漏检/生死转换下的存在性估计上有效，实现了精度与效率的同步提升。

Abstract: Transformer-based multi-target tracking (MTT) jointly learns data association and state estimation, but MT3/Track-MT3-style trackers repeatedly re-encode measurement windows, incurring redundant computation. We propose Causal Neural Set Filtering (CNSF)\footnote{\href{https://github.com/daihuangyu/CNSF}{Code: https://github.com/daihuangyu/CNSF}}, a neural set filter that encodes only current measurements while carrying past evidence in a structured recursive track state. CNSF combines exclusive Sinkhorn association, association-conditioned Kalman-shaped updates with moment matching, and recurrent Bernoulli lifecycle modeling with measurement-driven birth. These mechanisms impose soft one-to-one constraints, propagate association-induced state uncertainty, and support existence estimation under missed detections and birth--death transitions. On a held-out three-regime simulated test set, CNSF reduces mean GOSPA and T-GOSPA relative to Track-MT3 by 19.3\% and 30.4\%, with 55.9\% fewer parameters and a $3.76\times$ speedup in single-thread CPU inference.

</details>


### [83] [A Weighted Kernel Method for Approximation that Adapts to Learned Multivariable Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16606)
*John E. Darges,Laura Weidensager*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出总敏感度核（TSK），通过学习加权ANOVA核的输入权重因子来适应多变量黑盒函数结构，并证明解的唯一性与一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在输入重要性及其交互作用未知的情况下，仅凭有限数据逼近多变量黑盒函数的输入-输出行为十分困难。传统乘积核无法自适应目标函数的多变量结构。

Method: 构建基于加权ANOVA核族的总敏感度核（TSK），对目标函数各多变量分量的权重用每个输入的因子进行参数化；通过选择目标函数范数最小的再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）直接从函数观测中学习这些因子，并采用基于最小范数插值的有限数据方法。

Result: 在适当条件下证明该范数最小化问题存在唯一解，并建立了有限数据方法的一致性；学到的TSK因子可刻画各输入在主效应与交互作用中的参与程度，给出与总Sobol指数相关的、依赖核的输入敏感度度量。数值实验表明，适应学习到的多变量结构可显著提升逼近精度（相较标准乘积核）。

Conclusion: TSK通过自适应加权ANOVA核实现了对多变量结构的有效学习，兼具理论保证（唯一性与一致性）与实用价值（敏感度解释与精度提升）。

Abstract: Approximating the input-output behavior of a multivariable black-box function from limited data is challenging when blind to the importance of its inputs and their interactions. We introduce total sensitivity kernels (TSKs), a method based on families of weighted ANOVA kernels that learn and adapt to this multivariable structure. TSKs parameterize the weights on each multivariable component of the target function by factors for each input. We propose learning these factors directly from function evaluations by selecting the reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS) in which the target function has minimum norm. Under suitable conditions, we show that this norm-minimization problem admits a unique solution, and we establish consistency of a finite-data formulation based on minimum-norm interpolation. The learned TSK factors characterize the participation of individual inputs across interactions and main effects, providing a kernel-dependent notion of input sensitivity related to total Sobol indices. Numerical experiments demonstrate that adapting the kernel to learned multivariable structure can substantially improve approximation accuracy over a standard product kernel.

</details>


### [84] [Managing Action Preconditions in Neuro-Symbolic RL: Three Placement Strategies for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16056)
*Norbert Oswald,Fabian Deuser,Thomas Bräunl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将行为知识形式化为"结构动作"上的前置条件贝叶斯网络，并在RL回路的三处（推理端验证器、训练+推理强制器、学习器）注入该知识，在MiniGrid与Fetch上验证其提升解质量、样本效率与可追溯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类在新任务中直接复用熟悉情境的行为知识而非从头学习；RL智能体同样无需重新学习已知行为模式。神经符号RL可将符号知识注入学习策略，但注入位置至关重要：选点不当会产生幻觉式前置条件，导致智能体在变化环境中出现安全与可靠性问题。

Method: 将行为知识形式化为智能体"结构动作"（其合法性依赖前置条件，如拾取钥匙、抓取方块、开合门、放置物体）上的前置条件贝叶斯网络（BN），用于限制这些动作的触发条件，并以三种方式注入RL回路：(1) 符号验证器，仅在推理阶段调用，当前置条件满足时触发结构动作；(2) 符号强制器，在训练与推理阶段均生效，全程约束结构动作的使用；(3) 符号学习器，将知识折入网络，自行学习结构动作的限制与使用。在两个机制相反的基准（长序贯规划链与连续操作）上实验，并与强基线对比解质量、样本效率与可追溯性。

Result: 在MiniGrid上，三种注入方式均优于PPO+RND基线的解质量，其中符号强制器最高达98.2%，基线为88.8%。Fetch上的结果（原文省略）。

Conclusion: 在三处注入贝叶斯网络先验知识均能提升智能体表现，其中训练与推理全程生效的符号强制器效果最佳；知识注入位置是神经符号RL的关键设计选择，直接影响安全性与可靠性。

Abstract: Humans carry behaviour knowledge of how to act in familiar situations into every new task rather than relearning it from scratch. There is no reason a Reinforcement Learning (RL) agent shouldn't do the same: known behaviour patterns need not be learned, only applied. Neuro-symbolic RL bridges prior knowledge and RL by injecting symbolic knowledge alongside a learned policy. The point at which this knowledge is integrated is critical: a poor choice can produce, for instance, hallucinated preconditions, which surface as safety and reliability problems in agents acting in changing environments. We formalise this behavioural knowledge as a precondition Bayesian network (BN) over the agent's \emph{structural actions} - the actions whose legality depends on preconditions, such as picking up a key, grasping a block, toggling a door, or dropping an object. The BN restricts when these actions may fire, and we inject it into the RL loop at three placements: (1) a \emph{symbolic verifier}, consulted only at inference, that fires a structural action once its preconditions hold; (2) a \emph{symbolic enforcer}, active during both training and inference, that governs structural-action use throughout learning; and (3) a \emph{symbolic learner}, which folds the knowledge into the network and learns the restriction and use of structural actions itself. To test the three variants we run experiments on two benchmarks with opposite regimes: one built on long, ordered planning chains, the other on continuous manipulation. We compare against strong baselines on solution quality, sample efficiency, and traceability. The payoff is substantial. On MiniGrid, all three placements improve the \emph{solution quality} over the PPO+RND baseline, the symbolic enforcer leading at $98.2\%$ against the baseline's $88.8\%$. On Fetch, $\dots$

</details>


### [85] [High-Fidelity Digital Twin Data Models by Randomized Dynamic Mode Decomposition and Deep Learning with Applications in Fluid Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17101)
*Diana A. Bistrian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出从数值代码输出中以非侵入式技术构建高保真数字孪生数据模型（DTM）的新框架，将随机动态模态分解与深度学习人工智能相结合，并在三类复杂度递增的激波现象数值模拟中验证其精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望构建一种“降阶但高保真”的模型，用以镜像原始过程行为。其关键优势在于：对因动力学时间复杂而难以探索的工况，能在保证高精度的同时显著降低CPU时间与硬件成本。同时采用非侵入式方法，即不对控制方程进行伽辽金投影，从而提升方法的通用性与工程适用性。

Method: 将两种前沿工具融合：一是随机动态模态分解（randomized dynamic mode decomposition），二是深度学习人工智能，二者结合形成新的数字孪生数据模型构建框架。

Result: 所构建的DTM输出与原始源数据保持一致，且具备更低的复杂度；在三个复杂度递增的激波现象数值模拟中均得到验证，并对其数值精度与计算效率进行了系统评估。

Conclusion: 随机动态模态分解与深度学习的结合可有效生成高保真、低复杂度的数字孪生数据模型，适用于复杂动力学场景的数值模拟，兼具精度与计算效率优势。

Abstract: The purpose of this paper is the identification of high-fidelity digital twin data models from numerical code outputs by non-intrusive techniques (i.e., not requiring Galerkin projection of the governing equations onto the reduced modes basis). In this paper the author defines the concept of the digital twin data model (DTM) as a model of reduced complexity that has the main feature of mirroring the original process behavior. The significant advantage of a DTM is to reproduce the dynamics with high accuracy and reduced costs in CPU time and hardware for settings difficult to explore because of the complexity of the dynamics over time. This paper introduces a new framework for creating efficient digital twin data models by combining two state-of-the-art tools: randomized dynamic mode decomposition and deep learning artificial intelligence. It is shown that the outputs are consistent with the original source data with the advantage of reduced complexity. The DTMs are investigated in the numerical simulation of three shock wave phenomena with increasing complexity. The author performs a thorough assessment of the performance of the new digital twin data models in terms of numerical accuracy and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [86] [OmniHarness: Harnessing Generalizable Visual Generation via Symbolic Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16057)
*Xu Xu,Jinxiu Liu,Zhangbo Qiao,Jiaxing Lu,Xiangyu Zhang,Yubin Gu,Fangwei Ning,Yan Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 OmniHarness：通过符号策略学习实现可泛化的视觉生成，在执行中验证、在任务前自问练习，冻结模型参数持续扩展能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态大模型与多智能体系统虽推动了视觉生成，但仍存在三点不足：1) 现有方法蒸馏的任务经验泛化性有限；2) 反思往往延迟到任务完成之后；3) 知识仅在响应下游任务需求时才被获取。

Method: 将已验证的执行过程抽象为面向视觉生成任务族的符号策略，捕获共享流程与适用条件，剔除实例相关输入；harness 对新任务进行策略实例化、适配与组合。执行中通过中间验证指导修正与失败恢复；通过自主提问，在下游目标给定前自动生成并执行接近能力边界的练习任务；执行反馈持续精炼策略而模型参数保持冻结。

Result: 在 6 个基准、3 个 MLLM 骨干、3 个视觉智能体框架上取得强劲表现与持续能力扩展；ComfyBench Creative 任务解决率达 95.0%，超过最强基线 27.5 个百分点；冻结的策略快照可即插即用地提升现有视觉智能体系统。

Conclusion: 符号策略学习可在不改动模型参数的前提下实现可泛化、可复用、持续扩展的视觉生成能力。

Abstract: Unified multimodal large language models (MLLMs) and multi-agent systems have advanced visual generation. However, three limitations remain. (1) Existing methods often distill task-specific experience with limited generalizability. (2) Reflection is often deferred until task completion. (3) Knowledge is often acquired only in response to downstream task demands. To address these limitations, we introduce OmniHarness, a framework for generalizable visual generation via symbolic policy learning. OmniHarness abstracts verified executions into symbolic policies for visual generation task families, capturing shared procedures and applicability conditions while removing instance-specific inputs. The harness instantiates, adapts, and composes these policies for new tasks. Intermediate verification guides refinement and failure recovery during execution. Through self-directed inquiry, OmniHarness autonomously generates and executes practice tasks near its capability limits before downstream objectives are specified. Execution feedback continually refines the policies while model parameters remain fixed. Experiments across six benchmarks, three MLLM backbones, and three visual agent frameworks demonstrate strong performance and continual capability expansion. On ComfyBench's Creative tasks, OmniHarness achieves a 95.0% resolve rate, exceeding the strongest baseline by 27.5 percentage points. Frozen policy snapshots improve existing visual agent systems through plug-and-play reuse.

</details>


### [87] [Driver Behavior Estimation at Signalized Intersections Using a Physics-Constrained Decision-Conditioned Autoregressive Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16058)
*Mohammad Khoshkdahan,Pavel Laskov,Alexey Vinel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文分析并预测信号灯转换时人类驾驶员的决策与纵向轨迹行为，提出两阶段框架：先预测二分类操作决策，再用带物理约束的Transformer生成加速度轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红灯违规和信号交叉口的急刹车是交通事故的主要成因，因此需要理解并预测信号转换期间人类驾驶员的决策与纵向轨迹行为。

Method: 采集449次接近行为的真实数据集，使用RTK校正GNSS厘米级精度记录车辆运动，监测驾驶员心率和多级舒适度评分，并进行时空校准。统计分析发现所需减速度是停走决策的主导预测因子，对峰值减速度进行异方差高斯建模得到五个经验舒适区间。据此提出两阶段框架：阶段1预测二分类操作决策，阶段2使用决策条件自回归Transformer生成纵向加速度轨迹，并施加物理约束（目标状态条件与加加速度限制）。

Result: 所提架构优于基线方法，达到0.49m/s^2的加速度MAE和0.62m的距离MAE，并能从单个黄灯亮起时刻快照估计驾驶员未来停车舒适度水平；定性结果显示具有类人的真实制动行为。数据集与源代码已公开。

Conclusion: 该研究通过真实驾驶数据揭示了停走决策与减速度、舒适度之间的关系，并验证了两阶段建模框架在预测驾驶员决策与轨迹方面的有效性，为理解信号转换场景下的人类驾驶行为提供了数据与模型支持。

Abstract: Red-light violations and harsh braking at signalized intersections are major contributors to traffic accidents. This paper analyzes and predicts human driver decision-making and longitudinal trajectory behavior during traffic light signal transitions. We collected a diverse real-world dataset comprising 449 approach runs under varying speed and distance conditions. Vehicle motion was recorded using RTK-corrected GNSS with centimeter-level accuracy, and driver heart rate and multi-level comfort ratings were monitored. Spatial and temporal calibration ensured precise alignment between vehicle state and signal timing. Statistical analysis identifies required deceleration as the dominant single predictor of the stop-go decision, and heteroscedastic Gaussian modeling of peak deceleration reveals five empirical comfort ranges derived from human stopping behavior. Based on this insight, we propose a two-stage modeling framework. Stage 1 predicts the binary maneuver decision, and Stage 2 generates the longitudinal acceleration trajectory using a decision-conditioned autoregressive Transformer with physics constraints, including target-state conditioning and jerk limits. The proposed architecture outperforms baseline methods and achieves 0.49m/s^2 acceleration MAE and 0.62m distance MAE. It also estimates the future stopping-comfort level of the human driver from a single yellow-onset snapshot. Qualitative results demonstrate realistic human-like braking behavior. The dataset and source code are publicly available.

</details>


### [88] [HintMiner: Automatic Question Hints Mining From Q&A Web Posts with Language Model via Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16060)
*Zhenyu Zhang,JiuDong Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HintMiner，一种基于机器阅读理解与序列生成技术的自动问题提示挖掘工具，从海量在线问答帖子中为用户问题生成提示，在6万条Stack Overflow问题上取得BLEU 36.17%、ROUGE-2 36.29%的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线问答论坛（如Stack Overflow）难以及时、恰当地回应用户提问，用户需要一种自动化的方式来帮助其寻找答案。

Method: HintMiner利用机器理解与序列生成技术：先检索大量网络问答帖子，再用基于语言模型构建的MiningNet从中抽取提示。MiningNet是一个基于Transformer与复制机制的神经编码器-解码器模型，利用海量在线问答帖子设计自监督目标进行训练。

Result: 在60,000条Stack Overflow问题上的实验表明方法有效，平均BLEU得分为36.17%，平均ROUGE-2得分为36.29%，且工具与实验数据已公开。

Conclusion: HintMiner能够有效为用户问题自动生成提示，验证了结合机器阅读理解、序列生成与自监督学习的可行性。

Abstract: Users often need ask questions and seek answers online. The Question - Answering (QA) forums such as Stack Overflow cannot always respond to the questions timely and properly. In this paper, we propose HintMiner, a novel automatic question hints mining tool for users to help them find answers. HintMiner leverages the machine comprehension and sequence generation techniques to automatically generate hints for users' questions. It firstly retrieve many web Q\&A posts and then extract some hints from the posts using MiningNet that is built via a language model. Using the huge amount of online Q\&A posts, we design a self-supervised objective to train the MiningNet that is a neural encoder-decoder model based on the transformer and copying mechanisms. We have evaluated HintMiner on 60,000 Stack Overflow questions. The experiment results show that the proposed approach is effective. For example, HintMiner achieves an average BLEU score of 36.17\% and an average ROUGE-2 score of 36.29\%. Our tool and experimental data are publicly available.

</details>


### [89] [POSPAN: Position-Constrained Span Masking for Language Model Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16061)
*Zhenyu Zhang,Lei Shen,Yuming Zhao,Meng Chen,Xiaodong He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 POSPAN 框架，通过联合建模 span 长度分布与位置约束分布，统一并推广现有的 span 级掩码策略，显著提升预训练语言模型效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 span 级 MLM 仅考虑 span 长度的离散分布，忽略 span 之间的依赖关系，默认被掩码 span 位置均匀分布，导致掩码策略表达力受限。

Method: 提出 POSPAN 通用框架，将 span 长度分布与位置约束分布相组合，实现多样化的位置约束 span 掩码策略，并统一所有已有的 span 级掩码方法；在多个 NLU 基准数据集上进行预训练评估，并对位置约束进行理论分析。

Result: 实验表明位置约束能广泛增强 span 级掩码效果，最优 POSPAN 设置持续优于仅考虑 span 长度的对照方法及原始 MLM；理论分析进一步揭示了其有效性原因。

Conclusion: 位置约束是 span 级掩码的重要组成部分，POSPAN 兼具合理性与必要性，为 span 级掩码策略的设计提供了统一的通用框架。

Abstract: Span-level masked language modeling (MLM) has shown to be advantageous to pre-trained language models over the original single-token MLM, as entities/phrases and their dependencies are critical to language understanding. Previous works only consider span length with some discrete distributions, while the dependencies among spans are ignored, i.e., assuming that the positions of masked spans are uniformly distributed. In this paper, we present POSPAN, a general framework to allow diverse position-constrained span masking strategies via the combination of span length distribution and position constraint distribution, which unifies all existing span-level masking methods. To verify the effectiveness of POSPAN in pre-training, we evaluate it on the datasets from several NLU benchmarks. Experimental results indicate that the position constraint is capable of enhancing span-level masking broadly, and our best POSPAN setting consistently outperforms its span-length-only counterparts and vanilla MLM. We also conduct theoretical analysis for the position constraint in masked language models to shed light on the reason why POSPAN works well, demonstrating the rationality and necessity of POSPAN.

</details>


### [90] [Signed p-adic Residual Encodings of Finite-Domain All-Different Systems with a Sudoku Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16063)
*Greg Baker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究有符号、加权的仿射 p-adic 残差目标，将其作为有限域约束的原生编码。在分离有限字母表的素数下，充分加权的正一元行将每个系数钉在其允许集合中，负行奖励不等端点或子句满足。坐标支配定理将所有全局极小值置于有限域内，此时损失在加性常数意义下等价于 all-different 冲突计数或满足 CNF 子句数的负值。以标准数独作为 81 系数案例，无需 one-hot 提升，并提供客户端实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为有限域约束（如数独、CNF）寻找无需 one-hot 提升的原生编码，利用 p-adic 残差与加权符号行直接表示约束，从而避免编码膨胀并统一处理 all-different 与子句满足类问题。

Method: 采用有符号、加权仿射 p-adic 残差目标；选取能分离有限字母表的素数；用充分加权的正一元行约束系数取值，用负行奖励不等端点或子句满足；通过坐标支配定理保证全局极小值落在有限域内。

Result: 坐标支配定理表明每个全局极小值均位于有限域中；在该域内损失（至多加性常数）等于 all-different 冲突计数或满足 CNF 子句数的负值。标准数独给出 81 系数案例研究，无需 one-hot 提升，并配有客户端实现，公开生成的数据框、算术、诊断与搜索。

Conclusion: 有符号加权仿射 p-adic 残差目标可作为有限域约束的紧凑原生编码，在无需 one-hot 提升的情况下统一刻画 all-different 与 CNF 满足问题，数独案例验证了可行性并提供了可复现的客户端实现。

Abstract: We study signed, weighted affine $p$-adic residual objectives as native encodings of finite-domain constraints. For primes that separate the finite alphabet, sufficiently weighted positive unary rows pin each coefficient to its allowed set, while negative rows reward unequal endpoints or clause satisfaction. A coordinatewise domination theorem places every global minimiser in the finite domain; there the loss is, up to an additive constant, the all-different conflict count or the negative number of satisfied CNF clauses. Standard Sudoku provides an $81$-coefficient case study without a one-hot lift. A client-side implementation exposes the generated dataframes, arithmetic, diagnostics, and searches.

</details>


### [91] [You Don't Need To Train: Agentic Heuristic Learning Studio for Executable Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16065)
*Siyu Yuan,He Zhang,Sizhen Bian,Bin Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出AHL Studio，用"智能体启发式学习"替代反向传播来做人类活动识别（HAR），面向边缘部署生成可检查、可编辑、可回放的免大模型策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HAR通常被框定为神经网络基于梯度的训练，与人类"记例子、成规则、修错误"的认知式学习方式不同，作者希望探索这一互补视角。

Method: 提出AHL Studio工具，实现Agentic Heuristic Learning：学习阶段的智能体对传感器协议进行推理，提出可执行的启发式策略，记录修复轨迹，并导出用于边缘部署的免LLM策略；覆盖从数据观察到面向边缘导出的端到端流程，聚焦HAR基准族。

Result: 在迄今评估的11个HAR数据集上，AHL策略取得了较强的可执行策略性能，同时保持可检查、可编辑、可回放的特性。代码见https://github.com/zhaxidele/ahl-ts-studio。

Conclusion: AHL为HAR提供了一条非梯度、类认知学习的可行路径，其产出的策略兼具性能与透明性，适合边缘部署。

Abstract: Human activity recognition (HAR) is usually framed as gradient-based training of neural networks. Agentic Heuristic Learning (AHL) Studio explores a complementary view inspired by human cognitive learning: people learn activities by remembering examples, forming rules, and repairing mistakes, not by backpropagating. This proposed tool implements AHL for HAR: a learning-time agent reasons over sensor protocols, proposes executable heuristic policies, records repair traces, and exports an LLM-free policy for edge deployment. We focus on the HAR benchmark family and provide an end-to-end workflow from dataset observation to edge-oriented export. On eleven HAR datasets evaluated so far, AHL policies reach strong executable-policy performance while remaining inspectable, editable, and replayable \footnote{https://github.com/zhaxidele/ahl-ts-studio}.

</details>


### [92] [A panoramic aerodynamic performance prediction method for turbomachinery cascades using transformer-enhanced neural operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16066)
*Qineng Wang,Zhendong Guo,Liming Song,Tianyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"全景式"涡轮机械性能预测框架：不直接预测目标函数，而是先预测Navier-Stokes方程基本物理量（温度、压力、密度），再据此获得叶栅性能参数；并引入Transformer增强神经算子（TNO）提升精度，以Rotor 37为基准验证，误差优于FNO与DeepONet，下游任务计算成本降低四个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 涡轮机械设计中需要灵活、快速的气动性能评估。现有大多数预测模型直接回归所关心的目标函数，泛化性受限，难以像CFD一样对多样化的目标量进行预测，因此需要一种可替代CFD仿真的通用预测框架。

Method: 提出全景性能预测框架：首先预测Navier-Stokes方程的基本参数（温度、压力、密度），再基于这些基本物理量预测叶栅子午面关键性能参数，从而具备类似CFD模拟器的功能；框架内引入Transformer增强神经算子（TNO）以提高预测精度，并以Rotor 37叶片为参考，针对跨声速压气机叶片在子午面上的性能进行训练。

Result: TNO能准确预测等熵效率、质量流量等总体量以及总压比分布；其预测误差小于FNO、DeepONet等先进深度学习算子。将该模型应用于敏感性分析和多目标函数优化等下游任务时，性能接近CFD模拟器，同时使下游任务计算成本降低约四个数量级。

Conclusion: 所提TNO在解决不同类型下游任务上有效且可靠，验证了全景性能预测框架作为CFD替代工具的可行性与工程价值。

Abstract: To enable flexible and rapid aerodynamic performance evaluation in turbomachinery design, this paper proposes a panoramic performance prediction framework. Unlike most previous prediction models that directly predict the objective functions of interest, our approach first predicts the basic parameters of the Navier-Stokes equations, such as temperature, pressure, and density. Utilizing these basic physical quantities, it subsequently predicts key performance parameters of the turbine stage meridian plane. By adopting this methodology, our proposed panoramic performance prediction framework functions similarly to a CFD simulator, capable of predicting various objective of interest to the designers. To enhance prediction accuracy, a transformer-enhanced neural operator (TNO) is introduced within this framework. Using the Rotor 37 blades as a reference, the proposed TNO is trained to predict the performance of a transonic compressor blade in the meridian plane. The TNO can accurately predict total quantities such as isentropic efficiency, mass flow, and distributions of total pressure ratio. Remarkably, the prediction error of TNO is observed to be smaller than that of state-of-the-art deep learning operators such as the FNO and DeepONet. Furthermore, the TNO is applied to downstream tasks, including sensitivity analysis and optimization of various objective functions. The results confirm that the TNO can operate almost like a CFD simulator, while reducing the computational cost of downstream tasks by four orders of magnitude. The effectiveness and reliability of the proposed TNO for solving different kinds of downstream tasks have been well demonstrated.

</details>


### [93] [A Dynamic Aggregation Strategy Enhanced Efficient Global Optimization Algorithm for Solving High-Dimensional Turbomachinery Design Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16067)
*Qineng Wang,Zhendong Guo,Yun Chen,Guangjian Ma,Liming Song,Jun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出带动态聚合策略的高效全局优化算法DA-EGO，用于高维昂贵黑箱问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决维度d≥30的高维昂贵黑箱问题在有限预算下的优化困难。

Method: 将高维空间分解为低维子空间进行代理模型搜索，子空间最优解组合为精英点用于全局搜索；子空间变量每轮迭代依据子空间与全空间的变量交互分析动态更新，采用扰动法和方差分析检测变量交互；并根据上一轮子空间优化结果自适应调整搜索范围。

Result: 在21个基准测试（7个函数×30/60/90维，预算1500次评估）上对可分离及部分可分离问题有效；非可分离shifted Rosenbrock函数上优势依情形而定，60/90维时GSGA更优，30维时与IKAEA、GSGA统计相当；并在28变量跨音速转子叶片与60变量压气机级气动优化中体现优势。

Conclusion: DA-EGO的有效性得到充分验证。

Abstract: In order to solve the high-dimensional ($d \geq 30$) expensive black-box problems within budget, an efficient global optimization (EGO) algorithm with a dynamic aggregation strategy is proposed, labeled as DA-EGO. Specifically, the DA-EGO decomposes the original high-dimensional design space into a set of low-dimensional subspaces for efficient surrogate-based optimization search, and the optimal solutions of subspaces are combined as an elite point for the global search. Most importantly, the subspaces are not fixed. Instead, the subspace variables are updated in each iteration, according to the variable interaction analyses in the sub- and full-spaces. The perturbation method and the analysis of variance are used to detect variable interactions. To further accelerate the optimization progress, the searching ranges of subspaces are also adaptively adjusted according to the analyses of subspace optimization results of the previous iteration. Tests on 21 benchmark instances, comprising seven functions at 30, 60, and 90 dimensions, show that DA-EGO is effective on separable and partially separable problems under a budget of 1500 function evaluations. Its advantage is case-dependent: on the non-separable shifted Rosenbrock function, GSGA performs better at 60 and 90 dimensions, while the 30-dimensional results are statistically comparable to IKAEA and GSGA. Moreover, the advantage of DA-EGO is also seen in the aerodynamic optimization of a transonic rotor blade with 28 variables as well as the compressor stage optimization with 60 variables. With the above, the effectiveness of the proposed DA-EGO has been well demonstrated.

</details>


### [94] [Beyond Distribution Matching: Semantics-Consistent Tabular Diffusion with Weak Semantic Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16069)
*Yili Wang,Ruxue Shi,Mengnan Du,Hangting Ye,Yi Chang,Xin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出语义一致的表格扩散框架，将弱语义先验作为生成条件而非事后过滤，在保真度、语义一致性与下游效用上超越多种基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格生成器主要优化分布保真度，未显式建模表格模式与文本描述中编码的弱语义先验，因此生成的合成表格虽能匹配真实分布，却可能违反约束有效行的语义规则。

Method: 先借助LLM从元数据中抽取两类先验——列内语义与列间符号规则，并在真实训练集上验证；再将异构列值、列标识与语义先验映射到统一语义空间；随后执行按列的前向加噪与先验条件化的反向去噪，以同时保持边缘分布与规则一致的跨列依赖。

Result: 在六个真实表格基准上，相较代表性VAE、GAN、LLM与扩散类基线，一致提升分布保真度、语义一致性及下游任务效用；额外分析显示语义先验部分缺失时仍具鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将弱语义先验前置为生成条件，可有效弥补纯分布对齐的不足，实现语义一致的表格扩散生成，并在先验不完整场景下保持稳健。

Abstract: Synthetic tabular data can match real data distributions while still violating the semantic constraints that govern valid tabular rows. This reveals a key limitation of existing tabular generators: they mainly optimize distributional fidelity, but do not explicitly model weak semantic priors encoded in tabular schema and textual descriptions. In this paper, we propose \ours, a semantics-consistent tabular diffusion framework for high-fidelity synthetic data generation under weakly specified semantic priors. \ours\ first constructs two types of priors, namely intra-column semantics and inter-column symbolic rules, with LLM-assisted extraction from metadata and validation on the real training split. These priors are then used as generation conditions rather than post-hoc filters. Specifically, \ours\ maps heterogeneous column values, column identities, and semantic priors into a unified semantic space, and performs column-wise forward corruption and prior-conditioned reverse denoising to preserve both marginal distributions and rule-consistent cross-column dependencies. Extensive experiments on six real-world tabular benchmarks show that \ours\ consistently improves distributional fidelity, semantic consistency, and downstream task utility over representative VAE-, GAN-, LLM-, and diffusion-based baselines. Additional analyses further demonstrate the robustness of \ours\ when semantic priors are partially unavailable.

</details>


### [95] [Schema-Adaptive Action-Conditioned JEPA for Cross-Machine CNC Transfer under Partial Sensor Overlap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16071)
*Ayoub Louaye Bouaziz,Matthieu Ostertag,Anton Demasles*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究跨机床部署工业世界模型的问题，提出模式自适应动作条件联合嵌入预测架构（SAAC-JEPA），针对数控动力学场景中源机17通道与目标机仅10通道部分重叠的情形进行零样本迁移评估。结果显示源域预测精度无法反映目标域迁移能力，跨机床自适应构成独立的评测维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业世界模型跨机床部署需应对动力学、传感接口、采样机制与控制单元的差异。现有评估往往以源域预测精度为准则，但这一指标可能不足以反映模型在目标机上的实际预测表征质量，尤其在半重叠传感通道条件下缺乏明确的评测轴。

Method: 提出SAAC-JEPA，一种模式自适应、动作条件的联合嵌入预测架构。评测设计包含：组不相交的源域划分、仅源域归一化、留出自监督验证、单元审计，以及模型锁定后的密封目标测试。涉及五个随机种子的JEPA预训练对比、20个候选的源域搜索及七种子稳定性检查、预声明的配对消融（比较RevIN）与锁定前的自适应扫描。

Result: 五种种子下JEPA预训练未带来干净的源域预测增益：从头训练与预训练体模型RMSE分别为0.811±0.022与0.813±0.022。源域搜索在20个候选后选择模式一致的动作条件JEPA。确认性目标测试中，锁定模型零样本RMSE=0.546、R²=0.012、NLL=0.52，优于持续性基线但不及配备RevIN的PatchTST与iTransformer（0.503与0.498）。预声明配对消融显示同架构中RevIN在三种子下将RMSE改善至0.495±0.004，但在平稳上下文窗口上目标校准恶化（NLL=20.6）。锁定前自适应扫描将RMSE进一步降至0.520，但目标支持有限。

Conclusion: 源域预测精度本身不足以评估工业预测表征质量，跨机床在部分传感重叠下的自适应应被视为独立的评测轴。未来工作需关注目标侧校准与有限支持条件下的迁移策略。

Abstract: Cross-machine deployment of industrial world models requires transfer across changes in dynamics, sensing interfaces, sampling regimes, and control units. We study a schema-adaptive action-conditioned Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (SAAC-JEPA) for CNC dynamics, where the source machine has 17 canonical sensor channels and the target shares only 10. Evaluation uses group-disjoint source splits, source-only normalization, held-out self-supervised validation, unit audits, and a sealed target test after model locking. Across five seeds, JEPA pretraining gives no clean-source forecasting gain: scratch and pretrained-body models obtain \(\mathrm{RMSE}=0.811\pm0.022\) and \(0.813\pm0.022\). A source-only search over 20 candidates selects a schema-consistent action-conditioned JEPA after seven-seed stability checks. On the confirmatory target pass, the locked model reaches zero-shot \(\mathrm{RMSE}=0.546\), \(R^2=0.012\), and \(\mathrm{NLL}=0.52\), outperforming persistence but not RevIN-equipped PatchTST and iTransformer baselines (\(0.503\) and \(0.498\)). A pre-declared paired ablation shows that RevIN in the same architecture improves RMSE to \(0.495\pm0.004\) over three seeds, but degrades target calibration (\(\mathrm{NLL}=20.6\)) on stationary context windows. A pre-lock adaptation sweep further reduces RMSE to \(0.520\) with limited target support. These results show that source-domain forecasting accuracy alone is insufficient to assess industrial predictive representations, and that cross-machine adaptation under partial sensor overlap is a distinct evaluation axis.

</details>


### [96] [Pseudo-Label Augmentation for Affect Sensing in Small Collaborative Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16077)
*Meisam Jamshidi Seikavandi,Tanya Ignatenko,Fabricio Batista Narcizo,Paolo Burelli,Jesper Bünsow Boldt,Andrew Burke Dittberner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究利用GroupAffect-4四人协作数据集，在稀疏监督下比较多种伪标签增强策略用于生理情感感知，发现伪标签增强优于纯标注基线，但人格相似度范围过窄（0.91-0.99）使其主要起同队筛选作用而非校准信任信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然场景下群体交互的生理情感感知受限于标签稀疏而非传感器数据不足：可穿戴设备产生大量时间窗，而自我报告每场仅采集数次。因此作者希望通过伪标签增强来充分利用稀疏标注的协作情感数据。

Method: 基于GroupAffect-4四人协作数据集（含可穿戴生理信号、眼动、大五人格及任务后VAD标签），在统一的目标构建流程下比较四种策略：无增强、高斯过程伪标签、人格感知信任加权、人格+置信度联合加权；并评估跨被试LOSO与严格会话隔离LOGO两种迁移设置。

Result: 在已知团队设定下，伪标签增强优于纯标注基线。但大五人格余弦相似度范围狭窄（0.91-0.99），细粒度人格加权失效，人格相似度仅相当于同队筛选器。经平滑后，各增强SVM变体在Valence和Arousal上基本持平，而人格+置信度联合变体在Dominance上得分最高。跨被试LOSO迁移表现良好（尤其Arousal），而严格会话隔离LOGO则消除了增强收益。

Conclusion: 伪标签增强可更充分地利用稀疏标注的协作情感数据；人格信息最有价值之处在于作为队内选择机制。鉴于仅10个群体，LOGO应被视为未见群体迁移的保守下界。

Abstract: Physiological affect sensing in naturalistic group interaction is often limited by sparse labels rather than sensor data: wearable devices produce many time windows, while self-reports are collected only a few times per session. Using GroupAffect-4, a four-person collaborative dataset with wearable physiology, eye tracking, Big Five personality, and post-task VAD labels, we study pseudo-label augmentation for affect sensing under sparse supervision. We compare no augmentation, Gaussian Process pseudo-labelling, personality-aware trust weighting, and joint personality-plus-confidence weighting within a shared target-construction pipeline. Results show that pseudo-label augmentation improves over the labelled-only baseline in the known-team setting. However, the narrow range of Big Five cosine similarities (0.91-0.99) makes fine-grained personality weighting ineffective; personality similarity functions mainly as a same-team filter rather than a calibrated trust signal. With smoothing, augmented SVM variants are effectively tied on Valence and Arousal, while the joint personality-plus-confidence variant gives the highest Dominance score. Cross-subject LOSO transfer remains encouraging, especially for Arousal, whereas strict session-isolated LOGO removes the augmentation benefit. Given only 10 groups, LOGO should be interpreted as a conservative lower bound on unseen-group transfer. Overall, the results suggest that pseudo-label augmentation can make better use of sparsely labelled collaborative affect data, while personality information is most useful as a within-team selection mechanism.

</details>


### [97] [Distilling Foundation Models for Agentic What-If Reasoning:Cost, Latency, and Governance in a Hybrid LLM+SLM Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16091)
*Sourish Dey,Aditya Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将TabPFN教师模型蒸馏为紧凑前馈学生模型，参数压缩超6000倍，同时保持95%以上准确率与96%以上AUC，解决表格基础模型推理延迟高的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型通过上下文学习具备强零训练预测性能，但推理延迟高，难以作为交互式智能体循环中的热路径决策后端。

Method: 在UCI Adult及五个OpenML基准的业务决策模拟上，将TabPFN教师蒸馏为紧凑前馈学生模型；分类头参数由53.2M压缩至8,546（6,220倍），部署的双头贷款流水线由111.4M压缩至17,059（6,532倍）；并设置alpha=0硬标签对照实验。

Result: 学生模型保持95.4-100.5%准确率和96.8-100.0% AUC，最低准确率保留出现在credit-g（95.4%）；alpha=0硬标签对照显示教师软标签带来2.1-7.0个AUC点增益。

Conclusion: 蒸馏可将表格基础模型压缩为低延迟的紧凑学生模型，同时保持高预测性能；教师软目标相比硬标签具有显著增益。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models deliver strong zero-training predictive performance via in-context learning, but their high inference latency makes them impractical as hot-path decision backends in interactive agentic loops. We distill a TabPFN teacher into a compact feed-forward student across a business-decision simulation on UCI Adult and five OpenML benchmarks: the classification head compresses 53.2M parameters to 8,546 (6,220x); the deployed two-head loan pipeline compresses 111.4M parameters to 17,059 (6,532x). The student retains 95.4-100.5% accuracy and 96.8-100.0% AUC, with the lowest accuracy retention on credit-g at 95.4%; an alpha = 0 hard-label control shows that the teacher's soft targets provide a 2.1-7.0 AUC point gain.

</details>


### [98] [Evaluating Open-Weight E-Commerce Agents with Environment-Grounded Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16093)
*Nimit Shah,Haitz Sáez de Ocáriz Borde*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种确定性、可复现的电商购物对话评测环境，通过预先固定客户与轨迹参数、记录每步动作及环境状态，实现对会话各环节的细粒度验证与多维度评分，替代单一任务成功率指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 购物对话存在多条通向同一购物车的路径，传统任务成功率（task-success rate）将所有这些路径压缩成单一分数，掩盖了失败的具体原因，如行动不足、过度购买、无依据的产品属性、检索能力差等。需要一种能够保留证据、支持过程性评估的评测方法。

Method: 构建确定性、可复现的电商环境：预先固定每次试验的客户与轨迹参数（人格、难度、目标购物车、商品揭示时间表）。由模拟消费者在待评模型协助下尝试购买目标购物车；环境引导模拟器动作并记录每个助手动作及其对应的环境状态。试验后，评估者基于保留的证据对会话各部分打分，例如仅当客户已提及某产品时才因未呈现该产品而惩罚检索。利用证据对不同工具调用施加差异化惩罚（与预期工具调用集合比较）。环境与模拟器双向交互：读取模拟器输出，当客户过于沮丧时提前终止试验，并实时注入指令以指定何时探索、推迟购买或回顾先前交流。

Result: 在8个20B至35B参数的开源权重智能体上进行实验，每个智能体160次试验、44项指标。所得能力画像能够区分行动不足、过度购买、无依据的产品属性以及检索能力差等失败模式，而这些在终端成功率指标下均被掩盖。

Conclusion: 通过证据保留与过程性、可验证的开放式模拟评测，能够揭示被单一成功率掩盖的细粒度能力差异，为购物对话智能体提供更具诊断性的评估框架。

Abstract: A shopping conversation has many routes to the same cart, and a task-success rate reduces all of them to one score. We build a deterministic and reproducible e-commerce environment that precommits each trial's customer and trajectory parameters, including the persona, difficulty, target cart, and an item reveal schedule. A simulated consumer attempts to buy a target cart from the environment with assistance from the evaluated model. The environment guides the simulator's actions and records every assistant action alongside the environment state at that point. After the trial, these records allow the evaluator to assess individual parts of the conversation against the retained evidence. For example, the evaluator penalizes a search for failing to surface a target product only when the customer has already mentioned that product. We further use this evidence to apply different penalties to tool calls depending on how the assistant's actions compare with an expected tool-call set. Our environment also interacts with the simulator bidirectionally, reading its output to stop the trial when the simulator determines that the customer has become too frustrated and injecting directives in real time that specify when to explore, defer buying an item, or recall a previous exchange. This interaction creates an open-ended and verifiable simulation. Across eight open-weight agents from 20B to 35B parameters, with 160 trials per agent and 44 metrics, the resulting capability profiles distinguish under-action, over-purchase, unsupported product attributes, and poor search, all of which terminal success obscures.

</details>


### [99] [SWB-DM: A Calibrated Sliced-Wasserstein-Barycenter Aggregator with Delayed-Momentum Caching for Byzantine-Robust Federated Learning under Partial Participation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16099)
*Saranraj S,Saranya M S,Alex David S,Ajay Kumar A*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该摘要指出联邦学习鲁棒聚合方法的有限样本保证依赖"每轮参与者是总体无偏样本"这一脆弱假设，并提出SWB-DM：以切片级截尾Wasserstein重心、medoid规范固定与延迟动量缓存机制，将鲁棒性与采样偏差解耦，同时系统性刻画了多种防御的失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有坐标中位数、Krum、Bulyan、截尾均值等鲁棒聚合依赖"本轮出现的客户端是全体总体的公平样本"的隐含假设；在每轮仅少数客户端参与时，即使少量攻击者也能主导该样本，使有限样本保证静默失效。

Method: 提出SWB-DM：SWB将客户端更新的每个切片视为一维分布，跨客户端计算截尾Wasserstein重心，并通过medoid-based gauge-fixing恢复坐标身份（作者明确其为自研启发式，不属于标准最优传输理论）；DeMoA式延迟动量在每轮对全体客户端总体缓存更新，使鲁棒性与"恰好被采样者"解耦；并强调截尾比例标定非装饰性，欠截尾会在恰当标定可存活的污染水平下崩溃。

Result: 在448组CIFAR-10配置及CIFAR-100、FEMNIST与500客户端可扩展性实验中，发现若干机制上不同的失效模式：等样本坐标中位数退化为确定性错误答案；Krum静默违反n>2f+2前提并无预警地发散；Bulyan的n≥4f+3阈值产生尖锐的通过/失败边界。攻击方面，IPM比ALIE更可靠地击败序统计类防御（含SWB），并通过delta空间测量与收敛界加以验证。

Conclusion: SWB-DM的缓存带来真实预热成本，但在统一轮次预算下，其CIFAR-10增益显著偏大；CIFAR-100上FLTrust获益更多，但其原因与缓存完全无关。

Abstract: Robust aggregation methods for federated learning quietly rest on a fragile assumption: that whoever shows up in a given round is a fair sample of the full population. In practice, they rarely are. When only a handful of clients participate per round, even a modest fraction of adversaries can dominate that sample and silently invalidate the finite-sample guarantees that coordinate-wise median, Krum, Bulyan, and trimmed mean all depend on.
  We introduce SWB-DM to address this directly. SWB treats each slice of a client update as a one-dimensional distribution, computes a trimmed Wasserstein barycenter across clients, and recovers coordinate identity via a medoid-based gauge-fixing step -- a heuristic we developed and do not claim it belongs to standard optimal-transport theory. DeMoA-style delayed momentum then caches updates across the full client population each round, decoupling robustness from whoever happened to be sampled. Trim ratio calibration is not cosmetic: under-trimming causes collapse at corruption levels a properly calibrated model survives.
  Across 448 CIFAR-10 configurations, plus CIFAR-100, FEMNIST, and a 500-client scalability run, we find several mechanistically distinct failure modes. Even-sample coordinate-wise median degrades to a deterministic wrong answer. Krum silently violates its own n greater than 2f+2 precondition and diverges without warning. Bulyan's n greater than or equal to 4f+3 threshold produces a sharp pass/fail boundary. On attacks, IPM defeats order-statistic defenses -- including SWB -- more reliably than ALIE, confirmed through delta-space measurements against a convergence bound.
  SWB-DM's cache carries a real warm-up cost, but extending all baselines to the same round budget shows its CIFAR-10 gains are disproportionately large. On CIFAR-100, FLTrust benefits more -- for reasons entirely unrelated to caching.

</details>


### [100] [A Decision-Support Audit Protocol for Supervision Drift in Proxy-Labeled Credit-Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16102)
*Mehrdad Shoeibi,Muhammad Shabanpour,Waldemar Karwowski,Niloofar Yousefi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于代理标签信用风险模型监督漂移审计的"锁定式多信号审计协议"，通过五层诊断区分基率漂移、概率尺度漂移与特征-标签关系变化，并在LendingClub数据上验证其可行性与边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信用风险模型使用代理标签训练并在时间与人群分段变化下部署，但现有单一迁移度量无法区分基率漂移、概率尺度漂移与特征-标签关系变化，导致漂移诊断混淆、难以指导治理决策。

Method: 设计科学工件：锁定式多信号审计协议，包含五层诊断——迁移性能、oracle-gap探针、校准诊断、特征-标签稳定性、合成阳性对照，并预先锁定阈值与决策规则再行解读。在公开LendingClub数据（2013–2016时间迁移与跨分段迁移）上执行审计。

Result: 排序稳定性良好，oracle gap较小；最明显的时间信号是患病率与概率尺度失配，仅截距型诊断重校准即可大幅缓解，但其成因无法从现有数据版本中辨识；合成阳性对照仅对较大注入漂移有响应，更细微的漂移无法排除。

Conclusion: 该协议可产出有界的、可解释的漂移判读，而非单一结论；诊断模式到治理动作的映射仅为概念性指导，未经实证验证；细微漂移的不可排除性构成方法的重要局限。

Abstract: Credit-risk models are trained on proxy labels and deployed under temporal and segment change, yet no single transfer metric separates base-rate shift, probability-scale shift, and feature-label relationship change. We contribute a design-science artifact: a locked, multi-signal audit protocol for supervision drift in proxy-labeled credit-risk prediction. Five layers (transfer performance, an oracle-gap probe, a calibration diagnostic, feature-label stability, and a synthetic positive control), thresholds, and decision rules were locked before interpretation; a bounded reading is a designed outcome. On a public LendingClub dataset (temporal 2013 to 2016 and cross-segment transfer), ranking is stable and oracle gaps are small; the clearest temporal signal is a prevalence and probability-scale mismatch that intercept-only diagnostic recalibration largely reduces, though its cause is not identifiable from the available release. The positive control responds only to larger injected shifts; subtler drift cannot be excluded. Mapping diagnostic patterns to governance actions is conceptual guidance, not validated here.

</details>


### [101] [LLMs as Master Forgers: Generating Synthetic Time Series Data for Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16155)
*Mantek Singh,Jeshwanth Challagundla,Prateek Karnal,Gagan Ganapathy,Vineet Shah,Ridam Arora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用大语言模型（LLM）结合检索增强生成（RAG）技术生成制造业过程合成时间序列数据的框架，在量化指标、PCA分析和异常检测任务上均优于ARIMA和LSTM等传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实制造业场景中标注时间序列数据稀缺，制约了鲁棒机器学习模型的开发，因此探索LLM学习复杂时间依赖并生成逼真合成数据的潜力。

Method: 在制造业过程指令上微调预训练LLM，并采用RAG技术提升合成数据的多样性与真实性；通过量化指标、PCA分析和下游异常检测任务与ARIMA、LSTM等传统时间序列建模方法进行对比评估。

Result: LLM驱动框架优于ARIMA和LSTM等基线，生成的合成时间序列数据质量高，能有效捕捉真实制造数据的时间依赖与统计特性，并提升下游任务性能。

Conclusion: LLM结合RAG可有效缓解制造业标注数据稀缺问题，生成高质量合成时间序列数据并改善下游任务，为数据驱动的制造建模提供新思路。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel framework leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs) to generate synthetic time series data for manufacturing processes. Motivated by the scarcity of labeled time-series data in real-world manufacturing settings, which hinders the development of robust machine learning models, we explore the potential of LLMs to learn complex temporal dependencies and generate realistic synthetic data. Our approach involves fine-tuning pre-trained LLMs on manufacturing process instructions and employing a Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) technique to enhance data diversity and realism. We evaluate our method against traditional time series modeling techniques like ARIMA and LSTMs, using quantitative metrics, PCA analysis, and downstream task performance (anomaly detection). Results demonstrate that our LLM-driven framework outperforms these baselines, generating high-quality synthetic time series data that effectively captures temporal dependencies and statistical properties of real manufacturing data, leading to improvements in downstream task performance.

</details>


### [102] [LLM Inference in a Flash!](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16161)
*Sebastian Zhao,Minseo Kim,Coleman Hooper,Luca Manolache,Michael W. Mahoney,Yakun Sophia Shao,Kurt Keutzer,Amir Gholami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作面向Flash存内计算设备，提出整数量化与基于稀疏字典编码的KV cache压缩方法，实现LLM端到端推理并显著降低KV cache动态数据搬运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理请求正转向长序列与重负载（RAG、推理时计算扩展、长上下文），而内存容量与通信带宽的增速跟不上负载复杂度增长。Compute-in-Flash可通过将计算移至存储附近、利用SSD大容量来缓解带宽瓶颈，但该硬件不支持高精度浮点运算且写入耐久性有限，难以直接部署LLM。

Method: 提出面向Flash存内计算设备的推理算法：1）端到端仅整数量化方案，消除昂贵的浮点计算；2）针对写入耐久性限制，设计基于稀疏字典编码的字典式KV cache压缩策略，将每个KV向量表示为静态字典向量的线性组合。二者共同支持对模型权重与KV cache的Compute-in-Flash利用，并减少昂贵的数据传输。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B与Qwen-2.5-7B上，组合方法在精度退化有限的同时，将动态KV cache流量减少15倍。

Conclusion: 通过算法层面协同设计（整数量化+字典式KV cache压缩），可有效克服Flash存内计算设备不支持浮点与写入耐久性受限的问题，使模型权重与KV cache均受益于Compute-in-Flash，显著降低数据传输开销。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown impressive capabilities across a range of natural language processing tasks, and LLM inference has emerged as a critical workload for enabling downstream applications. The demands of serving LLM inference are becoming increasingly challenging as requests shift toward longer sequences and heavier inference, driven by retrieval-augmented generation, inference-time compute scaling, and long-context applications. Additionally, these challenges are compounded by hardware trends, as memory capacity and communication bandwidth are not scaling as fast as increases in workload complexity. Compute-in-Flash is a promising solution to address memory bandwidth limitations by moving computation close to memory, and to exploit the large capacity of SSD technologies. However, it is challenging to deploy LLMs on these systems as they lack support for high-precision floating point operations and have limited write endurance. In our work, we aim to address these challenges by designing inference algorithms to enable LLM inference on Flash compute-in-memory devices. We present an end-to-end integer-only quantization approach to eliminate expensive floating-point computations. To address the limited write endurance, we design a dictionary-based KV cache compression strategy based on sparse dictionary coding that represents each KV vector as a linear combination of static dictionary vectors. These algorithmic improvements enable us to exploit the benefits of Compute-in-Flash for both model weights and KV cache, and to minimize expensive data transfer operations. Across Llama-3.1-8B and Qwen-2.5-7B, our combined method exhibits limited accuracy degradation while reducing dynamic KV cache traffic by 15$\times$.

</details>


### [103] [Skeletal Prototypes on Iterative Nerve Expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16170)
*Jordan Eckert,Henry Schenck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SPINE（Skeletal Prototypes on Iterative Nerve Expansions），将原型约简中的类别模型从有限点集推广为嵌入的1-复形（图），边集由类别条件Mapper图初始化、顶点在分类目标下迭代拟合，观测由最近复形判定类别。在17个基准数据集、分层10折交叉验证下与7种原型约简方法在相同预算下比较，SPINE取得最高平均准确率与最佳平均排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统原型约简方法用较小的点集替代训练集，类别表示始终是有限点集，无法显式刻画局部簇之间的连接关系。作者希望让原型具备拓扑结构，使连接线段（图边）也参与分类决策。

Method: SPINE以嵌入的1-复形（图）表示每个类别。初始边集来自类别条件Mapper图，由数据决定哪些局部簇相互连接；后续阶段在分类目标下拟合顶点，样本分配给距离最近的类别复形，因此图的线段本身进入决策规则而不仅是拟合过程。

Result: 在17个基准数据集、分层10折交叉验证、相同预算下，SPINE取得最高平均准确率和最佳平均排名；经Wilcoxon符号秩检验（Holm校正）显著优于7个竞争者中的5个。预算扫描显示，在原型稀少时整图线段决策规则贡献最大，而整体方法在中等预算下表现最好；构造开销与判别式方法相当，并在17个数据集中的14个上快于广义学习矢量量化（GLVQ）。

Conclusion: 以1-复形替代点集作为原型表示是有效的，拓扑线段参与决策能在原型预算紧张时带来收益，SPINE在准确率、排名和计算效率上均具竞争力。

Abstract: Prototype reduction replaces a training set with a smaller representation, and the established methods return a finite set of points. We propose Skeletal Prototypes on Iterative Nerve Expansions (SPINE). The model for each class is an embedded 1-complex rather than a point set. Its initial edge set is a class-conditional Mapper graph, so the data decide which localized clusters are joined. Later phases fit the vertices under a classification objective, and an observation is assigned to the class whose complex is nearest. The segments therefore enter the decision rule and not only the fitting. We evaluate SPINE on seventeen benchmark datasets under stratified 10-fold cross validation, against seven other prototype reduction methods at a matched budget. SPINE attains the highest mean accuracy and the best average rank. It is significantly better than five of the seven competitors under Wilcoxon signed-rank tests with Holm correction. A budget sweep shows that the decision rule using the entire graph segments contribute most when prototypes are scarce, while the method as a whole competes best at moderate budgets. Construction cost places SPINE with the discriminative methods, and it is faster than generalized learning vector quantization on fourteen of the seventeen datasets.

</details>


### [104] [Z-Loss Backward Geometry in Dense Output Heads and Sparse Routers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16179)
*Bum Jun Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从反向传播视角重新分析Z-loss，提出"反向传输"框架，分离源幅度、softmax形状与传输因子，揭示相同前向Z-loss值可对应不同logit梯度与参数更新，并在GPT-2/Pythia上验证架构感知变体能减小反向几何尾部。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Z-loss此前仅被理解为对log-normalizer的标量惩罚，其梯度在架构与实现中的行为未被系统分析；而现代Transformer中大词表softmax头、top-k路由、融合损失与混合精度优化器相互交织，需要理解Z-loss反向传播的真实影响。

Method: 从backward-pass视角分析Z-loss，定义logit空间的"backward source"，构建backward-transport视角，将源的标量幅度与softmax形状同传输因子分离；传输因子涵盖common-shift坐标、tied-embedding路径、output-to-hidden增益、融合损失源一致性、优化器面向的更新及top-k路由归约尺度。

Result: 诊断表明近乎相同的前向Z-loss值可伴随不同的logit空间梯度，并经理架构与优化器传输后形成不同参数更新；解释了原始logit Z-loss能减小标量尾部却不改变output-to-hidden增益，以及active-route归约会改变有效路由系数。在GPT-2与Pythia系列、WikiText-103与FineWeb-Edu上的评估显示，架构感知变体在低系数区间减小反向几何尾部，同时保持可比的验证困惑度。

Conclusion: Z-loss应被视为嵌入架构与优化器传输链路中的梯度源而非简单标量惩罚；架构感知的Z-loss变体可在不牺牲验证困惑度的前提下改善反向几何，为混合精度与大词表训练提供更精细的正则化设计依据。
