Result: 信念状态可从残差流激活中线性解码，HMM与LLM各组合的探针峰值R²为0.83–0.99，且分布于从早层到晚层。对探针子空间进行修补和引导后的下游预测质量接近未干预模型，而对照条件性能显著下降，说明该表征具有功能相关性。

Conclusion: 结果提供了表征层面的证据：开源LLM的ICL近似于对上下文推断出的生成模型进行最优贝叶斯预测。该发现将"输入分布结构决定激活几何"的结论从显式训练的玩具网络推广到生产级大模型。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) trained on next-token prediction exhibit remarkable in-context learning (ICL) abilities, yet the representations that support ICL remain poorly understood. We consider such representations in a controlled setting: prompting LLMs with data emitted from hidden Markov models (HMMs) and probing for the corresponding belief state -- the posterior distribution over the HMM's hidden states given the observed token history. Across six open-source LLMs prompted with data from 40 HMMs selected for non-trivial belief structure, we find that belief states are linearly decodable from residual stream activations, with peak probe $R^2$-values from 0.83-0.99 across HMM and LLM combinations, ranging from early to late layers. To establish functional relevance, we intervene directly on the probe-identified subspace via patching and steering, resulting in downstream prediction quality on the order of the untampered model, while controls degrade performance substantially. Together, these results provide representation-level evidence that ICL in open-source LLMs approximates optimal Bayesian prediction over a context-inferred generative model. More broadly, our findings extend prior results linking input-distribution structure to activation geometry: from toy networks trained explicitly on HMM data to production-scale LLMs.

</details>


### [173] [OPEN-1B: A Fully Auditable Training Run](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17380)
*John Donaghy,Brian Wilcox,Oğuzhan Ersoy,Shikhar Rastogi,Adam St Arnaud,Alexey Titov,Jordan Greenberg,Ben Fielding,Harry Grieve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 开源语言模型因浮点运算的非结合性而缺乏可证可复现性；本文提出"完全可审计"透明度层级，通过对训练非确定性来源施加确定顺序，实现跨异构商用硬件的逐比特可复现，并支持集体审计方案，同时发布Open-1B模型及全套审计资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开源模型虽公开权重、数据与训练配方，但因浮点运算非结合性导致无法被"可证"复现。框架的确定性模式仅限同机同硬件，无法跨硬件验证已发布检查点是否真由声明配方产生，从而留下未披露数据、注入偏见或后门等风险，现有proof-of-learning与proof-of-training-data技术无法排除。

Method: 提出新的"完全可审计"透明度层级，要求训练中每个样本上的每个操作都能在异构商用硬件上以比特级确定性独立复现。具体通过对三类训练非确定性来源施加确定顺序：GPU kernel归约、数据并行集群中的数据批序、节点间/节点内集合通信；从而使大型分布式训练的任意单步可在单块商用硬件上重放并与已发布轨迹比对。因单机重放整个训练不可行，辅以集体验证方案，由多个独立审计者各自认证单步、共同覆盖全程。

Result: 实现了任意训练步骤在异构商用硬件上的比特级可复现与验证；发布Open-1B模型，以及完整预训练数据集、所有中间检查点、训练代码库和用于复现与验证其训练任一步骤的审计工具。

Conclusion: 该工作将模型透明度提升到可审计层级，使检查点与声明训练配方之间可被比特级验证，从而排除未披露数据、偏见注入或后门等隐患，并通过集体审计与Open-1B全套资源为开放模型的可复现性提供可操作范式。

Abstract: Open-source language models have a reproducibility problem. Despite releasing weights, training data, and recipes, none of them are provably reproducible due to the non-associativity of floating-point arithmetic. Deep learning frameworks often offer a deterministic execution mode, allowing reproducible operations on the same machines. Unfortunately, this determinism does not carry across hardware such that a user can verify that a released checkpoint was actually produced using the declared training recipe. This leaves room for undisclosed data, injected biases, or backdoors that existing techniques such as proof-of-learning or proof-of-training-data cannot rule out.
  We introduce a new tier of model transparency, fully auditable, in which every operation on every data sample during training is independently reproducible on heterogeneous commodity hardware with bitwise certainty. By imposing a definite order on the sources of training nondeterminism, GPU kernel reductions, data batch ordering across a data-parallel cluster, and inter/intra-node collective communication, we make it possible to replay any individual step of a large, distributed training run on a single piece of commodity hardware and check it against the published trajectory.
  Because replaying an entire run on one machine is infeasible, we support this with a collective verification scheme in which many independent auditors each certify individual steps, together covering the whole run. We release Open-1B, a model trained under this regime, together with its full pretraining dataset, every intermediate checkpoint, the training codebase, and the audit harness needed to reproduce and verify any step of its training.

</details>


### [174] [Bridging the Confidence Gap: Temperature Scaling for Calibrating Test-Time Prompt Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17386)
*Yuwei Liang,Jian Liang,Dapeng Hu,Yinuo Xu,Ran He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出CoTS与E-CoTS两种后处理校准方法，通过对适应预测施加温度缩放以缩小其与零样本预测的置信度差距，在保持甚至提升准确率的同时显著降低校准误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时提示调优（TPT）虽能在单个测试样本上取得更高准确率，却往往牺牲校准性能；已有校准方法多通过引入跨文本嵌入的分散正则项来降低校准误差，但常导致准确率下降。作者受到零样本预测本身校准良好的启发，试图寻找一种既能校准又不损失准确率的方法。

Method: 提出CoTS：对适应后的预测应用温度缩放，最小化其与零样本预测之间的置信度差距，属于后处理校准。为进一步利用适应过程中的多次增强，引入弱-强集成策略以提升准确率，并将CoTS应用于该集成得到E-CoTS，以保持其良好校准特性。

Result: 在多种数据集与骨干网络上，方法在不大幅损害主任务准确率的前提下有效缓解了误校准。例如在ImageNet变体上，E-CoTS将TPT的平均期望校准误差（ECE）从11.90%降至5.38%，同时准确率从60.74%提升至62.95%；与现有校准方法结合时，E-CoTS通常能同时提升准确率与校准。

Conclusion: CoTS与E-CoTS是一种简单有效的后处理校准方案，可保持并提升TPT的准确率，且能与已有校准方法互补，同时改善准确率与校准性能。

Abstract: Test-time prompt tuning (TPT) enables adaptation on a single test instance, achieving improved accuracy but often sacrificing calibration performance. Most existing calibration methods introduce additional regularization terms to promote dispersion across text embeddings and reduce calibration error, yet these methods often suffer from a drop in accuracy. Motivated by the well-calibrated nature of zero-shot predictions, we propose CoTS, a simple yet effective post-hoc calibration method that preserves accuracy. Specifically, CoTS applies temperature scaling to minimize the confidence gap between adapted and zero-shot predictions. To fully exploit the potential of multiple augmentations during adaptation, we introduce a weak-strong ensemble strategy that further boosts accuracy. We then apply CoTS to this ensemble, termed E-CoTS, to maintain its well-calibrated property. Extensive experiments on diverse datasets and backbones show that our approaches effectively mitigate miscalibration without compromising primary accuracy. For instance, E-CoTS reduces the average expected calibration error of TPT from 11.90% to 5.38% on ImageNet variants, while even increasing accuracy from 60.74% to 62.95%. Moreover, when integrated with existing calibration methods, E-CoTS usually enhances both accuracy and calibration simultaneously.

</details>


### [175] [Knowledge as Orbit: Finite Collections as Phases of an Exactly Periodic Latent Generator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17417)
*Siddharth Pal,Viktoria Rojkova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出用"有限循环动力学"实现知识的集约式（intensional）存储：将X个条目编码为学习潜空间中固定旋转的第i个相位，用共享网络解码所有相位，并将潜变量推进建立在整数谐波旋转库（实数离散傅里叶算子）上，从而保证R^X=I的精确闭合并非学习得到。实验表明精确周期算子在图像与循环视频上优于一般学习算子与非周期保范算子，容量受解码器预算约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规的有限知识存储是外延式的（extensional），即每个条目对应一个独立码或向量，存储开销随条目数线性增长。作者希望探究：一个有限集合能否改为以集约式（intensional）方式存储，即通过一个紧凑"定律"（law）的解码轨道来表示，且该定律能精确回到起点（exact recurrence）。这一动机指向用"动力学"而非"独立实例"来承载有限知识，以降低潜变量存储成本并保证循环闭合的精确性。

Method: 1) 将第i个对象编码为学习潜空间中固定旋转的第i个相位，所有相位由共享网络解码；2) 潜变量通过一组处于循环整数谐波上的旋转（实数离散傅里叶算子）推进，使R^X等于单位阵，精确闭合由代数保证而非学习；3) 以图像为受控载体，循环视频视为相位顺序本身即为内容时间结构的情形；4) 设置消融：固定解码器，仅更换算子（一般学习算子、保范但非周期算子、精确周期算子），并考察解码器容量（稠密解码器 vs. 小型卷积解码器）与codebook对照。

Result: 固定解码器仅更换算子时：一般学习算子发散；保范但非周期算子在循环处退化；精确周期算子表现平稳，且在真实图像上差距进一步扩大。容量方面：稠密解码器对每张清晰图像存在结构性开销，任何规模都无法与压缩调和；而小型卷积解码器在共享流形的对象上可同时达到清晰与压缩。codebook对照显示生成定律在重构意义上"免费"，但会使潜变量存储成倍增加。在七个基准视频片段上，与匹配的帧索引基线相比，循环方法在相同参数下达到相当或更好的保真度，并以机器精度完成回绕，而基线留下可见接缝；将基线频率固定到循环谐波后其接缝也消失，证实精确周期性是关键约束。

Conclusion: 有限循环知识可以以动力学而非独立实例的形式存储：精确递归由代数提供，内容由共享解码器提供。该范式在循环视频等相位顺序与内容时间结构一致的任务上尤其自然，并揭示了精确周期性是循环闭合的决定性约束，同时表明解码器容量（而非潜变量规模）才是清晰度与压缩权衡的核心。

Abstract: Finite knowledge is usually stored extensionally, one code or vector per item. We ask whether a finite collection can instead be stored intensionally, as the decoded orbit of one compact law that returns exactly to its start. For X objects, we encode item i as the i-th phase of a fixed rotation in a learned latent space and decode all phases with a shared network; the latent advances through a bank of rotations at integer harmonics of the cycle, a real discrete Fourier operator, so that R^X equals the identity and exact closure is guaranteed rather than learned. Images are a controlled carrier; looping video is the case where the phase order is the content's own temporal structure. Holding the decoder fixed and varying only the operator, a general learned operator diverges, a norm-preserving but non-periodic one degrades around the loop, and the exactly periodic operator is flat; on real images the gap widens. Capacity is then the decoder's budget: dense decoders carry a structural overhead per crisp image that no size reconciles with compression, while a small convolutional decoder on objects that share a manifold reaches crisp and compressed. A codebook control shows the generative law is free in reconstruction terms while multiplying the latent store many-fold. On seven benchmark clips, against a matched frame-index baseline, the cycle reaches equal or better fidelity at equal parameters while wrapping at machine precision, where the baseline leaves a visible seam; pinning the baseline's frequencies to loop harmonics closes its seam too, confirming that exact periodicity is the operative constraint. Finite cyclic knowledge can be stored as dynamics rather than independent instances, with exact recurrence supplied by algebra and content by a shared decoder.

</details>


### [176] [Learning-Guided Planning in Large Dynamic Action Spaces: Budgeted Tree Search for One-to-Many Mobile Charging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17429)
*Liang-Ching Tao,Pi-Chung Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LP-BTS学习引导规划架构，通过图提议策略、价值评论家与边预算PUCT，在动态大规模动作空间（736–2,813个充电站）中实现单一冻结检查点泛化，取得最高观测生存率与alive-AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接"状态→动作"映射在候选动作数量多、具几何结构且随状态重建时表现脆弱。一对多移动充电场景中，N=250传感器初始即诱导约1,125个候选充电停靠动作，且随传感器死亡动作空间动态变化，需要能在大型动态动作空间中进行学习引导规划的方案。

Method: LP-BTS由三部分组成：(1)图提议策略将候选支持集聚焦到小规模；(2)学习价值评论家评估叶节点；(3)边预算PUCT比较短期模拟未来后再提交动作。策略不依赖固定输出头，故单一冻结检查点可覆盖所有评估设置。设计匹配消融实验考察各组件贡献。

Result: 均匀采样导致生存率损失8.8个百分点；在固定目标支持集下，PUCT联合保留1.4个百分点（约250个传感器中的3.5个），而直接策略选择行程远23%。在预设封存30场景确认集（仅评一次）上，LP-BTS达到最高生存率0.4545与alive-AUC 0.8031；相对最强领域工程基线的生存优势为+0.0066（95% CI [-0.0037, +0.0184]），差异未决；但在每个配对场景上均超过截止期启发式与两个源派生直接策略重建。两个学习行均为Gong等所报变体的源派生重建训练结果。

Conclusion: 在该设置下，结果为大型动态动作空间中的学习引导规划提供了受控证据；LP-BTS总体优于启发式与直接策略重建，但与最强领域工程基线差异尚未被统计确认。

Abstract: Many learned sequential decision systems map the current state directly to an action. That shortcut becomes brittle when candidate actions are numerous, geometrically structured, and rebuilt with the state. One-to-many mobile charging makes this setting concrete: with N=250 sensors, the initial state induces about 1,125 candidate charging-stop actions; each chosen stop simultaneously serves its in-range sensors, and the action universe changes as sensors die. LP-BTS is a learning-guided planning architecture: a graph proposal policy concentrates a small candidate support, a learned value critic evaluates leaves, and edge-budgeted PUCT compares short simulated futures before committing an action. Because the policy scores this set without a fixed output head, a single frozen checkpoint covers every evaluated setting, spanning action universes from 736 to 2,813 stops. Matched ablations reveal complementary effects: uniform sampling costs 8.8 survival percentage points, while, with targeted support fixed, PUCT jointly retains 1.4 points (about 3.5 of 250 sensors) and direct policy selection travels 23% farther. On a prospectively specified, sealed 30-scenario confirmatory bank evaluated once, LP-BTS attains the highest observed survival (0.4545) and alive-AUC (0.8031). Its estimated survival advantage over the strongest domain-engineered comparator is +0.0066 (95% CI [-0.0037, +0.0184]), an unresolved difference, while it exceeds a deadline heuristic and two source-derived direct-policy reconstructions on every paired scenario. Both learned rows are trained, source-derived reconstructions of variants reported by Gong et al. In this setting, the results provide controlled evidence about learning-guided planning in a large, dynamic action space.

</details>


### [177] [Reduced-Space Multi-Fidelity Bayesian Optimization of Process Simulation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17440)
*Niki Triantafyllou,Andrea Bernardi,Maria M. Papathanasiou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RS-MFBO框架，将全局敏感性分析降维与多保真度高斯过程结合，用于高维昂贵的工业流程模拟优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业流程流程表优化因严格模拟成本高和设计空间维度灾难而计算代价巨大，需要一种能在有限高保真度评估预算下高效求解高维黑箱优化问题的方法。

Method: 该方法融合全局敏感性分析（GSA）进行维度约简，并采用保真度增强的高斯过程建模低保真近似与高保真评估之间的相关性；同时设计成本感知的采集策略，辅以冷却与提升机制，自适应地分配不同保真度下的采样。

Result: 在SuperPro Designer的质粒DNA生物过程和Aspen HYSYS的绿色燃料合成装置两类工业模拟器上，针对多种经济与物理目标进行验证；结果显示该方法在保持与单保真度基线相当的优化性能的同时，显著减少了高保真度模拟器评估次数。

Conclusion: RS-MFBO是一种可扩展、与模拟器无关的框架，适用于成本受限的黑箱优化，能够有效应对高维昂贵工业流程优化问题。

Abstract: Optimizing industrial process flowsheets is often computationally prohibitive due to the high cost of rigorous simulations and the curse of dimensionality inherent in complex design spaces. To address these challenges, we present a reduced-space multi-fidelity Bayesian optimization (RS-MFBO) framework designed for high-dimensional, expensive black-box functions. The approach integrates Global Sensitivity Analysis (GSA) for dimensionality reduction with a fidelity-augmented Gaussian process that captures correlations between low-cost approximations and expensive high-fidelity evaluations. A cost-aware acquisition strategy, augmented with cooldown and promotion mechanisms, adaptively guides the allocation of samples across fidelities. The framework is validated on two distinct industrial process simulators: a plasmid DNA bioprocess in SuperPro Designer and a green fuel synthesis plant in Aspen HYSYS. Results across diverse economic and physical objectives demonstrate that the proposed method substantially reduces the number of high-fidelity simulator evaluations while maintaining competitive optimization performance compared to single-fidelity baselines. These results highlight RS-MFBO as a scalable, simulator-agnostic approach for cost-constrained black-box optimization.

</details>


### [178] [Coupled Calibration and Learning: Mitigating Teacher Bias in LLM Distillation without Target-Domain Reward Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17474)
*Haichen Hu,Yuheng Zhang,David Simchi-Levi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出耦合校准与学习（CCL）算法，在仅有源域奖励反馈、存在协变量偏移的条件下，通过迭代校准教师并更新学生，使学生收敛到最优学生策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM蒸馏旨在将强大教师的能力迁移到小学生模型，但直接模仿会一并迁移教师的系统性偏差与错误；在协变量偏移下教师对目标任务可靠性不确定，且目标域奖励反馈不可用，这一挑战尤为突出。

Method: CCL通过对token级分支将教师校准与学生更新耦合：每轮先用源域反馈校准教师，再用校准后的教师在目标问题上训练学生；更新后的学生反过来为下一轮校准提供信息。采用自回归策略框架，并从理论上分析投影学生梯度更新的进展，同时控制教师校准误差。

Result: 理论上证明输出学生与oracle学生之间的期望平均KL散度以多项式速率收敛到零；oracle在学生类内最大化真实参考正则化目标奖励，无需代表无约束最优策略。进一步建立与正则化直接匹配的分离：即使教师的正则化目标奖励高于所有学生策略，直接匹配相对oracle学生的误差仍可有界远离零。

Conclusion: 结果表明，通过耦合校准与学习，LLM蒸馏可在无目标域奖励反馈的情况下克服持续的教师偏差并恢复最优学生策略。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) distillation aims to transfer the capabilities of a powerful teacher to a smaller student. Direct imitation, however, can also transfer the teacher's systematic bias and errors. This challenge is particularly pronounced under covariate shift, when the teacher's reliability on target questions is uncertain and target-domain reward feedback is unavailable. We propose Coupled Calibration and Learning (CCL), an LLM distillation algorithm that couples teacher calibration with student updates through token-level branching, using reward feedback only on source questions. Each iteration calibrates the teacher using source feedback and then uses the calibrated teacher to train the student on target questions. The updated student, in turn, informs subsequent calibration. In an autoregressive policy framework, we prove that the output student's expected average Kullback-Leibler divergence to the oracle student converges to zero at a polynomial rate in the number of iterations. The oracle maximizes the true reference-regularized target reward within the student class, which need not represent the unrestricted optimal policy. Our analysis quantifies the progress of projected student gradient updates while controlling the error in teacher calibration. We further establish a separation from regularized direct matching: its error relative to the oracle student can remain bounded away from zero even when the teacher achieves higher regularized target reward than every student policy. These results demonstrate that LLM distillation can overcome persistent teacher bias and recover the optimal student through coupled calibration and learning, without target-domain reward feedback.

</details>


### [179] [FreqSpaNet: Frequency and Spatial Learning of SFPF for Physical Layer Hardware Integrity Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17491)
*Xiaoxuan Huang,Jinlong Xu,YiZhe Wang,Meng Zhang,Xian Li,Yuying Bian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 FreqSpaNet，一种面向开放集硬件异常检测的时空频极化指纹（SFPF）表征学习网络。模型通过频率分支与几何感知空间分支分别建模频率局部变化与方向关系，经自适应融合与互补预训练整合两类表征。实验达到 96.31% 的平均 AUROC，较基线提升 9.05 个百分点，并在七种硬件替换场景下验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未授权的硬件替换可在保持无线设备逻辑身份不变的同时改变其物理实现，对硬件完整性验证构成威胁。时空频极化指纹（SFPF）虽能刻画设备在多个频率和方向上的依赖响应，但其频率维度与空间维度具有不同的结构依赖性，直接建模存在困难。

Method: 提出 FreqSpaNet 网络：频率分支捕获相邻频率间的局部变化；几何感知空间分支利用角度信息建模方向关系；两路表征通过自适应融合进行组合；并采用互补预训练在捕捉共享信息的同时保留频率与空间表征的差异性特征。整体面向开放集硬件异常检测。

Result: FreqSpaNet 取得 96.31% 的平均 AUROC，较基线高出 9.05 个百分点；在七种硬件替换场景下进一步验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 针对 SFPF 频率与空间维度结构依赖各异的问题，FreqSpaNet 通过双分支建模、自适应融合与互补预训练实现有效的表征学习，在开放集硬件异常检测任务上显著优于基线，并具备多场景鲁棒性。

Abstract: Unauthorized hardware replacement can preserve a wireless device's logical identity while altering its physical implementation, posing a challenge to hardware integrity verification. Spatio-frequency polarization fingerprints (SFPFs) capture device-dependent responses across multiple frequencies and directions, but their frequency and spatial dimensions exhibit different structural dependencies. We propose FreqSpaNet, an SFPF representation learning network for open set hardware anomaly detection. A frequency branch captures local variations among neighboring frequencies, while a geometry-aware spatial branch models directional relationships using angular information. The two representations are combined through adaptive fusion, and complementary pretraining further captures shared information while preserving the distinct characteristics of the frequency and spatial representations. Experiments show that FreqSpaNet achieves a mean AUROC of 96.31\%, 9.05 points above the baseline. Results under seven hardware replacement scenarios further verify the effectiveness of FreqSpaNet.

</details>


### [180] [ENCP: Episode-Normalized Conformal Prediction for Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17499)
*Vicky Feliren,A. Taufiq Asyhari,Muhamad Risqi U. Saputra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Episode-Normalized Conformal Prediction (ENCP)，通过对非一致性分数按策略残差置信度重缩放、并对每个 episode 校准单一最大分数，解决了 VLN 中因步骤依赖和变长 episode 导致的 conformal prediction 覆盖率失效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLN 模型的不确定性估计对识别模糊、不可靠预测及实现安全导航决策至关重要。作为先进的不确定性估计框架，conformal prediction (CP) 有望用于 VLN，但 VLN 智能体需要多步序列决策，标准 CP 校准无法在其所承诺的、依赖且变长的 VLN episode 上提供覆盖率保证。

Method: 提出 Episode-Normalized Conformal Prediction (ENCP)：将非一致性分数按策略的残差置信度（residual confidence）进行重缩放，并对每个 episode 校准一个最大分数。在可交换（exchangeable）的校准与测试 episode 假设下，该方法允许 episode 内各步骤之间存在依赖关系。

Result: 在 R2R 和 REVERIE 数据集上，针对四种 VLN 策略和三种非一致性分数，ENCP 在 seen-to-unseen 评估中均达到所有报告的实证逐步覆盖率（step-coverage）目标。

Conclusion: 在可交换校准与测试 episode 条件下，ENCP 能以至少 1−α 的概率覆盖每一步的真实值，同时允许 episode 内步骤间依赖。结果表明 ENCP 可提供模型无关的不确定性估计，有助于判断 VLN 智能体何时应交由更强预测器（包括人工协助）处理。

Abstract: Uncertainty estimation for Vision-Language-Navigation (VLN) models is a critical task since it can help identify ambiguous and unreliable predictions, enabling agents to make safer navigation decisions. As one of the most advanced uncertainty estimation frameworks, conformal prediction (CP) offers a promising approach for uncertainty estimation in VLN. However, given that VLN agent requires a sequence of steps, standard calibration in conformal prediction fails to provide coverage guarantee it promises over a dependent, variable-length VLN episode. To this end, we propose Episode-Normalized Conformal Prediction (ENCP), which rescales a nonconformity score by the policy's residual confidence and calibrates one maximum score per episode. Under exchangeable calibration and test episodes, this construction covers the ground truth at every step with probability at least $1 - α$, while allowing dependence among steps within an episode. Across four VLN policies and three nonconformity scores on R2R and REVERIE dataset, ENCP meets all reported empirical step-coverage targets on the seen-to-unseen evaluation. These results demonstrate that ENCP can provide model-agnostic uncertainty estimates, which might be useful for determining when a VLN agent should defer to a more capable predictor, including human assistance.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [181] [Coaching Qwen3 Coder 30B to Think Like a CodeClash Arena Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16096)
*Ivy Ning Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文以CodeClash代码竞技场为测试平台，针对排名靠后的开源Qwen3-Coder-30B，提出ReAct SFT与轨迹质量加权SFT，用强智能体蒸馏知识提升弱代码智能体的多轮决策能力，微调后模型在锦标赛中超越原始Qwen3 Coder Plus。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱模型/开源权重代码智能体在长周期、多步交互任务中难以可靠理解用户意图：频繁产生语法与协议违反错误，且跨轮策略适应能力弱。作者追问：如何改善弱代码智能体的"思考过程"。由于离线SFT无法直接验证生成动作是否合法或有益，常规指令微调难以纠正此类失败。

Method: 在CodeClash（含6个竞技场、多轮锦标赛的代码竞技场基准）中开展案例研究，以Qwen3-Coder-30B为对象，蒸馏更强智能体的知识。提出两种方法：(1) ReAct SFT——将教师轨迹重写为显式的[obs][thought][act]链，强化观察-思考-动作的推理结构；(2) 轨迹质量加权SFT——对样本重新加权，鼓励编辑后的检查（post-edit checking）行为。

Result: ReAct SFT显著改善了策略行为；微调后的模型在锦标赛评测中优于原始Qwen3 Coder Plus。分析还确认Qwen3-Coder-30B未针对竞技场式交互优化，存在频繁的语法与协议破坏性错误及跨轮策略适应不足。

Conclusion: 通过将强智能体轨迹转化为显式[obs][thought][act]链并结合轨迹质量加权的SFT，可有效提升弱代码智能体的战略推理与交互可靠性，超越强商业模型基线，为弱代码智能体的"思考过程"优化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large language model coding agents have recently become useful for software tasks, but weaker or open-weight agents still struggle to reliably interpret user intent and execute complex multi-step workflows. This gap is especially visible in long-horizon settings, where an agent must repeatedly inspect prior outcomes, diagnose failure, and choose the next code edit under interaction constraints. It motivates a natural question: what can we do to improve the thinking process of a weak code agent? We study this question in CodeClash, a code-arena benchmark where the original work evaluates 8 commercial coding agents across 6 arenas through multi-round tournaments. Since Qwen3 Coder Plus ranks last among them, we take the open-weight Qwen3-Coder-30B as a case study and investigate how to improve it with distilled knowledge from stronger agents. Our analysis shows that Qwen3-Coder-30B is not well optimized for arena-style interaction: it frequently produces syntax and protocol-breaking errors and exhibits weak strategic adaptation across rounds. These failures are difficult to correct with vanilla instruction tuning alone, since offline SFT cannot directly verify whether a generated action is valid or beneficial. To address this, we propose ReAct SFT, which rewrites teacher trajectories into explicit [obs][thought][act] chains, and trajectoryquality weighted SFT, which reweights samples to encourage post-edit checking. ReAct SFT substantially improves strategic behavior, and our fine-tuned model outperforms the original Qwen3 Coder Plus in tournament evaluation.

</details>


### [182] [Docker Containers vs. Virtual Machines: A Comparative Study of Architecture, Performance, Configuration, and Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16148)
*Faraz Gurramkonda,Akanksha Malla,Sayma Tamboli,Shayesta Nazneen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对虚拟机与Docker容器进行文献综述式对比，从架构、配置与生命周期、性能、可扩展性及安全等维度分析，认为效率与隔离构成设计权衡，并指出容器运行于加固虚拟机中的混合架构是云与多租户场景的务实平衡方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代应用平台需在隔离工作负载的同时保持部署速度、可移植性、资源效率与安全性。虚拟机与容器在不同抽象层实现该需求，但二者在效率与隔离上的取舍缺乏系统性对比，故本文旨在梳理相关研究。

Method: 采用基于文献的对比分析方法，从架构、配置与生命周期管理、性能、可扩展性以及安全五个维度，对虚拟机和Docker容器进行系统性比较。文中强调容器性能优势受工作负载特征、存储与网络驱动、资源控制及实验设计等因素影响。

Result: 既有研究普遍表明容器启动更快、镜像更小、工作负载密度更高，且在多数场景下接近原生执行性能；而虚拟机开销更大，但提供独立内核、异构客户操作系统以及更强的隔离边界。

Conclusion: 效率与隔离应被视为设计权衡，而非断言某一技术普遍优越。容器运行于加固虚拟机内的混合架构，可为云与多租户系统提供实际可行的平衡方案。

Abstract: Modern application platforms must isolate workloads while preserving deployment speed, portability, resource efficiency, and security. Virtual machines (VMs) and Docker containers address this requirement at different abstraction layers: VMs virtualize hardware and run independent guest operating systems, whereas containers isolate processes while sharing the host kernel. This paper presents a comparative, literature-based analysis of the two approaches across architecture, configuration and lifecycle management, performance, scalability, and security. Published studies generally associate containers with shorter startup times, smaller images, higher workload density, and near-native execution for many workloads. These benefits depend on workload characteristics, storage and network drivers, resource controls, and experimental design. VMs introduce greater overhead but offer independent kernels, heterogeneous guest operating systems, and a stronger isolation boundary. The comparison therefore treats efficiency and isolation as a design trade-off rather than declaring one technology universally superior. A hybrid architecture, in which containers run inside hardened VMs, often provides a practical balance for cloud and multi-tenant systems.

</details>


### [183] [Models as Governed Interfaces for AI-Native MBSE: Read-Side Adequacy and Write-Side Admissibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16252)
*Jason Gower,Michael J. de C. Henshaw,Siyuan Ji*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出"认知充分性"(epistemic adequacy)作为AI参与系统工程的数据架构属性，以公开的Apollo 11 SysML v2模型为案例，将其拆为读侧充分性与写侧可准入性两半、共五条判据，并给出治理式查询架构框架(GQAF)，同时设定可证伪的评估方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可机读建模语言(如SysML v2)的程序化访问常被视为AI参与系统工程的充分条件，但作者认为访问是必要而非充分。真正的障碍不在建模语言，而在其周边的数据架构：结构完整的模型仍缺少推导链、认知状态标注、溯源信息以及可解析的证据；面对这些缺口，AI读者不会拒答，而会用训练数据填补——该来源既不可验证也不受治理。选取Apollo 11 SysML v2重建模型这一"符合当前最佳实践而非存在缺陷"的案例，恰恰是为了证明问题源于架构而非模型质量。

Method: 以公开的Apollo 11 SysML v2重建模型为探针案例，定义"认知充分性"并拆解为五条判据：四条位于读侧(由案例与趋同文献佐证)，第五条位于参与侧，作为尚未检验的假设提出。沿"内联元数据扩展→基底原生多模型存储"的架构空间，提出Governed-Query Architecture Framework (GQAF)，通过工程师既有的视点(viewpoint)约定治理智能体参与。

Result: 读侧的四条判据得到案例证据与文献趋同的双重支持；参与侧的第五条判据尚属未验证假设。架构方案以GQAF形式给出，界定从轻量内联元数据到基底原生多模型存储的选项谱系。

Conclusion: 作者将该重框定为可证伪研究议程：若认知层在同一模型上无法优于检索增强(RAG)基线，则视为被证伪；验证先在Apollo推导链上进行，随后进入工业试点。

Abstract: Machine-readable models such as SysML v2 are now programmatically accessible, and a growing body of work treats that access as the enabling condition for AI participation in systems engineering. Access is necessary, but not sufficient. The remaining work lies not in the modelling language but in the data architecture around it. An AI reader that queries a structurally complete model for a derivation still runs into absent derivation chains, untagged epistemic status, missing provenance, and evidence that the model cannot resolve. Faced with these gaps, it does not abstain; it fills them from training data, a source that is neither verifiable nor governed. To make the case on a model that is exemplary by current practice rather than deficient, we probe the public Apollo 11 SysML v2 reconstruction. We name the missing property epistemic adequacy and offer it as a candidate data-architecture pattern in two halves. Read-side adequacy lets derivation, status, and provenance answer a query rather than invite a guess; write-side admissibility gates an AI contribution before it enters the record. The property is broken down into five criteria. Four sit on the read side, evidenced by the case and convergent literature; the fifth sits on the participation side, advanced as a hypothesis this paper does not yet test. The architecture space runs from an inline metadata extension up to a substrate-native multi-model store, and over it, we propose the Governed-Query Architecture Framework, which governs agent participation through the viewpoint conventions that engineers already use. We commit the reframing to falsification: the epistemic layer counts as refuted if it cannot beat a retrieval-augmented baseline on the same model, tested first on the Apollo chain and then in an industrial pilot.

</details>


### [184] [AgentGuard: Learning Execution Guardrails from Anomalous Coding-Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16287)
*Wuyang Dai,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 AgentGuard 指令级护栏框架，从编码智能体的异常轨迹中学习条件执行约束，将异常执行率从 69.0% 降至 26.7%，任务成功率从 21.7% 提升至 35.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 编码智能体依赖执行环境与仓库、外部工具交互，但任务成功并不代表执行可靠：智能体可能修改无关文件、重写测试、发出不安全命令或忽略失败的校验。因此需要行为护栏来保障执行的可靠性，而人工设定安全规则成本高、覆盖有限。

Method: AgentGuard 是一个指令级护栏框架，从编码智能体的异常轨迹中学习条件式执行约束。它不依赖人工规则，而是自动抽取反复出现的执行失败模式，将其泛化为指令级行为约束，并组织为一个轻量级“护栏技能”，仅在当前指令相关时才动态激活相应规则，从而在提供行为引导的同时减少对正常执行的不必要限制。

Result: 使用来自 382 个仓库任务、642 条真实编码智能体执行失败轨迹进行评估。护栏从覆盖 282 个任务的 461 条轨迹中学习，在不相交的 100 个任务上测试。以 Claude Code + Claude Haiku 4.5 为底座智能体，对比基线智能体与加入 AgentGuard 的同一智能体：异常执行率由 69.0% 降至 26.7%，成功任务完成率由 21.7% 提升至 35.0%。

Conclusion: 实验表明，从历史失败中学习得到的执行护栏能显著提升 AI 编码智能体的可靠性，同时说明在安全性与任务完成率之间取得平衡仍是待解决的挑战。

Abstract: AI coding agents increasingly rely on execution harnesses to interact with repositories and external tools. However, task success does not guarantee reliable execution. Agents may still modify unrelated files, rewrite tests, issue unsafe commands, or ignore failed validations, motivating behavioral guardrails for reliable execution. We present AgentGuard, an instruction-level guardrail framework that learns conditional execution constraints from anomalous trajectories of coding agents. Rather than relying on manually specified safety rules, AgentGuard automatically extracts recurring execution failure patterns, generalizes them into instruction-level behavioral constraints, and organizes them as a lightweight guardrail skill that dynamically activates only the rules relevant to the current instruction.
  This design enables behavioral guidance while minimizing unnecessary restrictions on normal execution. We evaluate AgentGuard using 642 documented failure traces collected from real coding-agent executions across 382 repository tasks. Guardrails are learned from 461 traces covering 282 tasks and evaluated on a disjoint set of 100 tasks. Using Claude Code with Claude Haiku 4.5 as the underlying coding agent, we compare the baseline agent with the same agent augmented by AgentGuard. Experimental results show that AgentGuard reduces the Abnormal Execution Rate from 69.0% to 26.7% and increases the Successful Task Completion Rate from 21.7% to 35.0%. These results demonstrate that execution guardrails learned from historical failures can substantially improve the reliability of AI coding agents while highlighting the remaining challenge of balancing safety and task completion.

</details>


### [185] [Assurance Envelopes for Autonomous Coding Agents: Minimum-Cost Evidence for Software Change](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16302)
*Anjan Goswami*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文研究编码代理在复用既有软件证据时的最小恢复问题，提出"任务条件保证包络"概念，并用类型化推理图与闭包验证方法求解最小证据子集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码代理回到既有软件时会继承测试、类型检查、证明、静态分析与执行轨迹等先前工程证据。全部重载浪费资源，但遗漏变更所依赖的证据会导致必要性质失去支撑。因此需要确定：在给定变更必须保持的性质（义务）下，哪一最小成本证据子集能重新确立这些义务。

Method: 将证据及组合规则建模为类型化推理图（typed inference graph）；义务在从所选证据出发的前向链式推导到达时被满足。用该闭包（closure）验证每个选择，而非依赖优化器结果。评估数据来自先前AI编码代理运行所保留的软件衍生图，冻结这些工件后求解后续任务应恢复哪些累积证据。并构建249个实例的预设合成基准刻画计算特性，与CP-SAT优化器交叉校验。

Result: 来自Rust、IronBlocks与Pong结果的小图显示：最小包络依赖具体任务；当现有证据无法重新确立所需性质时可能不存在包络；部分性质需要多份证据联合支撑；扩展需求会追加证据而非替换证据。合成基准中，丢弃"多证据联合"结构的基线必然无法重新推导这些性质；所有完成的精确交叉校验均与CP-SAT优化器一致；500证据规模图的求解时间中位数低于20ms，但每个目标含大量替代推导路径的图在更小规模即超时，说明难度由结构而非原始规模决定。

Conclusion: 贡献在于将成熟优化方法有界地应用于为软件变更选择保证上下文。发现义务本身以及下游代理收益仍是未解决的问题。

Abstract: When a coding agent returns to existing software, it inherits evidence from earlier engineering work: tests, type checks, proofs, static analyses, and traces. Reloading all of it is wasteful, but dropping a piece the change depends on can leave a required property unsupported. Given the properties a change must preserve, its obligations, we ask which least-cost subset of the available evidence re-establishes them, and we call such a subset a task-conditioned assurance envelope. Evidence and the rules that combine it form a typed inference graph; an obligation is met when forward chaining from the selected evidence reaches it, and we validate every selection by that closure rather than by trusting the optimizer. The software-derived graphs in our evaluation come from preserved outcomes of prior AI coding-agent runs; we freeze those artifacts and ask which accumulated evidence should be restored for a later task. Small graphs from Rust, IronBlocks, and Pong outcomes show that the minimum envelope depends on the task, that none may exist when current evidence cannot re-establish a required property, that some properties need several pieces of evidence together, and that expanding the requirements adds evidence rather than replacing it. A prespecified synthetic benchmark of 249 instances characterizes computation: a baseline that discards the 'several pieces together' structure necessarily fails to re-derive them; every completed exact cross-check agreed with the CP-SAT optimizer; and median solve time stayed below 20 ms at 500-evidence graphs, except that graphs with many alternative derivations per target timed out at far smaller sizes, so structure, not raw size, drives difficulty. The contribution is a bounded application of established optimization to selecting assurance context for a software change; discovering the obligations and downstream agent benefit remain open.

</details>


### [186] [FairLint-DL: An IDE-Native Tool for Fairness Debugging of Deep Learning Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16321)
*Archit Rathod,Saeid Tizpaz-Niari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FairLint-DL：一个VS Code扩展，通过对表格数据集的预训练、IDE原生偏见检测实现公平性测试的左移，基于QID信息论指标评测三个基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平性分析工具多为训练后评估框架，需完成整个模型开发生命周期才能评估偏见，导致发现公平性问题过晚、修复成本高。作者希望将公平性测试左移，在数据阶段即实现偏见检测。

Method: FairLint-DL以可配置的深度神经网络作为代理模型，在表格数据集上直接检测偏见；采用基于Shannon熵与最小熵的信息论定量个体歧视（QID）指标量化受保护属性对预测的因果影响；实现两阶段梯度引导搜索算法以发现歧视性实例，因果调试流水线通过敏感性分析将偏见定位于特定网络层与神经元，并用SHAP和LIME双解释引擎进行特征级归因。

Result: 在三个表格基准（Adult Census Income、German Credit、Bank Marketing）上评测，公平性表现跨数据集差异显著：Adult上96.0%的实例QID超过0.1比特显著性阈值，平均QID为0.619比特，差异性影响比率为0.581，违反五分之四法则。对缓存模型可在12秒内产出结果。

Conclusion: FairLint-DL证明了将公平性分析集成到开发者工作流的可行性，且不会带来显著开销，为公平性测试左移提供了实用工具支持。

Abstract: Existing fairness analysis tools predominantly operate as post-training evaluation frameworks, requiring practitioners to complete the full model development lifecycle before assessing bias. We present FairLint-DL, a Visual Studio Code extension that implements a shift-left approach to fairness testing by enabling pre-training, IDE-native bias detection directly on tabular datasets. FairLint-DL trains a configurable deep neural network as a proxy model and applies information-theoretic Quantitative Individual Discrimination (QID) metrics. Grounded in Shannon and min-entropy, QID quantifies the causal influence of protected attributes on predictions. The system implements a two-phase gradient-guided search algorithm for discovering discriminatory instances, a causal debugging pipeline that localizes bias to specific network layers and neurons via sensitivity analysis, and dual explainability engines using SHAP and LIME for feature-level attribution. Evaluation on three tabular benchmarks (Adult Census Income, German Credit, and Bank Marketing) reveals fairness concerns that vary widely across datasets: on Adult, 96.0% of analyzed instances exhibit QID above the 0.1-bit significance threshold, with a mean QID of 0.619 bits and a disparate impact ratio of 0.581, violating the four-fifths legal rule. FairLint-DL produces these results within 12 seconds on cached models, demonstrating the feasibility of integrating fairness analysis into the developer workflow without significant overhead.

</details>


### [187] [Protocol-Preserving Context Trimming for Agentic Workflows: Benefits, Failure Regimes, and Budget Guardrails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16461)
*Harish Gaggar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究对比五种上下文修剪策略，发现保留协议关键状态比最大化裁剪token更能保障多步智能体工作流的可靠性，自适应预算护栏综合表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体LLM系统依赖长交互历史保存指令、工具状态与未决依赖，但无限制的上下文增长会增加计算成本并可能降低效率。研究旨在评估在可靠性约束下，协议保持型上下文修剪对多步智能体工作流的有效性。

Method: 对比五种修剪策略：基于近期性、基于相关性、摘要、协议感知修剪、自适应预算护栏，在不同保留上下文水平与工作流复杂度类别下评估。指标包括任务成功率、协议遵守度、有效工具调用、token节省、延迟降低、级联失败和关键上下文阈值。

Result: 传统策略平均token节省约60%，但任务成功率较低（66.6-77.3%），协议遵守度为85.5-88.6%。协议感知修剪将任务成功率提升至92.2%；自适应护栏达到96.0%任务成功率、96.3%协议遵守度、1.0%级联失败，平均token节省56.0%。保留上下文预算≤25%时失败几率是≥50%预算的10.92倍（p<0.001）。在激进预算下协议感知修剪成功完成的几率是传统方法的5.24倍，自适应护栏较固定协议感知修剪再提升2.11倍（p<0.001）。关键上下文阈值随工作流复杂度增加。

Conclusion: 可靠的上下文缩减更取决于保留协议关键状态，而非最大化token移除；自适应护栏可提升长时程智能体系统的效率、可扩展性与可靠性。

Abstract: Agentic large language model (LLM) systems rely on long interaction histories to preserve instructions, tool states, intermediate decisions, and unresolved dependencies, but unrestricted context growth increases computational cost and can reduce efficiency. This study evaluates protocol-preserving context trimming as a reliability-constrained approach for multi-step agentic workflows. Five trimming strategies - recency-based, relevance-based, summarization, protocol-aware trimming, and adaptive budget guardrails - were compared across retained-context levels and workflow-complexity classes using task success, protocol adherence, valid tool calls, token savings, latency reduction, cascading failures, and critical context thresholds. Conventional strategies achieved about 60% mean token savings but lower task success (66.6-77.3%) and protocol adherence (85.5-88.6%). Protocol-aware trimming improved task success to 92.2%, while adaptive guardrails achieved 96.0% task success, 96.3% protocol adherence, and 1.0% cascading failure with 56.0% mean token savings. Retained-context budgets of 25% or less increased failure odds 10.92-fold relative to budgets of 50% or more (p < 0.001). Protocol-aware trimming produced 5.24-fold greater odds of successful completion than conventional methods under aggressive budgets, while adaptive guardrails further increased success odds 2.11-fold versus fixed protocol-aware trimming (p < 0.001). Critical context thresholds also increased with workflow complexity. These findings indicate that reliable context reduction depends more on preserving protocol-critical state than on maximizing token removal, and that adaptive guardrails can improve efficiency, scalability, and reliability in long-horizon agentic systems.

</details>


### [188] [AI Policies: Help or Hindrance? A Software Developer's Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16496)
*Samuel Ferino,Rashina Hoda,John Grundy,Christoph Treude,Hashini Gunatilake*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 基于19份软件开发者访谈，揭示AI政策对开发者的帮助与阻碍，并提出以开发者为中心的AI政策引入建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件组织为缓解LLM相关风险（如敏感信息泄露、未授权使用）而制定的AI政策，若开发者不参与、不遵循则形同虚设。因此需要理解开发者对AI政策的实际体验，以提升政策的有效性。

Method: 采用定性研究方法，对19名软件开发者进行访谈，分析AI政策如何帮助和阻碍开发者的日常工作。

Result: 识别出AI政策对开发者的双重作用：既提供一定的指导与保护（帮助），也带来限制与负担（阻碍）。

Conclusion: 提出面向管理者与决策者的建议，倡导以开发者为中心的方式来引入AI政策，以提升开发者的参与度和政策的实际效用。

Abstract: AI policies introduced by software organisations to mitigate LLM-related risks such as sensitive information leaks and unauthorised usage are not useful if software developers do not engage with them. We draw on 19 software developer interviews to show how AI policies help and hinder developers. We suggest approaches to support managers and decision makers with a developer-centric approach to introducing AI policies.

</details>


### [189] [ExecuCritic: Calibrated Critic Shaping for Code Generation with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16604)
*Junjie Cao,Yingjie He*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ExecuCritic：用同一执行rollout联合训练coder与critic，critic预测通过/失败并给诊断反馈，仅在critic与executor一致时驱动coder，从而改善信用分配、减少梯度步与沙箱执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 执行反馈（单元测试）客观且直接衡量程序正确性，是代码模型的有效监督信号；但其弱点在于整个程序常被压缩为单一的通过/失败位，导致RLVR面临困难的信用分配问题。此外，现有编码系统虽常配有独立的审查者或测试者角色，但这些critic多依赖提示而非训练，且未与执行结果校准。

Method: 提出ExecuCritic联合训练框架：coder与critic在同一批执行rollout上同步更新；critic预测通过/失败结果并给出简短诊断反馈；仅当critic与executor在当前rollout组上达成一致时，coder才使用该信号。

Result: 在八个代码基准和两个近期开源backbone上，ExecuCritic优于无critic的GRPO、提示式审查者系统以及标量奖励模型基线，同时所需策略梯度步数更少、沙箱执行次数更少。

Conclusion: 消融与可靠性分析表明，性能提升来自更好的信用分配，而非更大的采样预算。

Abstract: Execution feedback is a useful supervision signal for code models because unit tests are objective and directly measure program correctness. Its weakness is that an entire program is often reduced to one pass or fail bit, leaving RLVR to solve a difficult credit assignment problem. At the same time, coding systems often include separate reviewer or tester roles, but these critics are usually prompted rather than trained and are not calibrated against execution. We propose ExecuCritic, a joint training framework in which a coder and a critic are updated on the same execution rollouts. The critic predicts pass or fail outcomes and gives short diagnostic feedback; the coder uses this signal only when the critic agrees with the executor on the current rollout group. Across eight code benchmarks and two recent open backbones, ExecuCritic improves over GRPO without a critic, prompted reviewer systems and scalar reward model baselines, while requiring fewer policy gradient steps and fewer sandbox executions. Ablations and reliability analyses suggest that the gains come from better credit assignment rather than larger sampling budgets.

</details>


### [190] [An Exploratory Study of Dependabot Cooldown Adoption in Open-Source GitHub Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16605)
*Hidetake Tanaka,Rikuto Tsuchida,Kazumasa Shimari,Raula Gaikovina Kula,Kenichi Matsumoto*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究实证考察GitHub热门开源仓库对Dependabot cooldown功能的采纳与配置情况及其动机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化依赖更新可能在维护者与社区察觉前迅速传播恶意软件包版本；2025年7月GitHub将Dependabot cooldown正式发布作为供应链攻击防御手段，但其早期采纳效果尚不清楚。

Method: 探索性实证研究：分析热门开源GitHub仓库对该功能的采纳与配置方式，并调查其采纳动机。

Result: 在92个已知动机的采纳事件中，83个由安全关切驱动；75个仅出于安全的采纳中有43个由安全lint警告触发。在保留cooldown的仓库内251个生态系统中，97.2%设置了通用延迟，其中64.3%采用7天，而各更新类型细粒度设置的使用率均低于10%。

Conclusion: 早期采纳者偏好简单的默认延迟而非细粒度控制，提示工具应提供反映生态支持状况的稳健默认值，并将细粒度控制留给具有明确更新优先级的依赖。

Abstract: Automated dependency updates can rapidly propagate malicious package releases before maintainers and the broader community have enough time to detect them. In July 2025, GitHub made Dependabot cooldown generally available as a defense against software supply chain attacks. However, the effects of its early adoption remain unknown. In this exploratory study, we empirically examine how popular open-source GitHub repositories adopt and configure the feature and investigate their motivations. We find that security concerns motivated 83 of 92 adoption events with known motivations. Security linter warnings triggered 43 of 75 security-only adoptions. Among 251 ecosystems within repositories that retained cooldown, 97.2% set a general delay. Of these, 64.3% used seven days, while use of each update type setting was below 10%. Early adopters therefore favor simple default delays over fine-grained controls. These findings suggest that tools could provide robust defaults reflecting ecosystem support and reserve fine-grained controls for dependencies with clear update priorities.

</details>


### [191] [Memory-Skill Isomorphism: One Skill Carrier, Two Native Uses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16669)
*Kang Ruiyuan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出以Skill作为记忆与技能的统一渐进式披露载体（L0描述/L1 SKILL.md/L2 reference），共享读侧、治理化地分离写侧，并在一个已部署系统中给出定价与存在性信号，主张"单一Skill载体族+共享读+治理化差异写"的RSI设计规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆与技能均能在不改权重的前提下提升智能体，但分别构建记忆与技能的存储、路由、检索、反思与更新路径会导致复用机制随积累而重复膨胀。作者观察到技能本身已是蒸馏记忆的天然载体，因此动机是消除这套重复的复用机制，用统一的载体承载记忆与能力。

Method: 将记忆组件实现为一个Skill，采用渐进式披露的三级结构：常驻description承载热线索（L0），按需加载的SKILL.md承载较冷的索引与策展策略（L1），reference/*.md承载详细记忆（L2）；以1,024字符的受治理description预算压缩常驻索引。反思可演化任一Skill。写侧分叉：追加历史、重写并重新验证当前状态。评估针对常驻索引实现，在定价操作点上比较首轮token、k=1与k=5的检索、会话总量与价格分解，并做小样本对比与单任务存在性检验。

Result: 治理是最关键的识别点：存在4条写入条目，但仅1/4进入结算账本。在定价操作点上，一条L1 lesson-1条目增加1,313首轮token，基线为1,462；k=1时基本持平（1,313对1,365），k=5时为4,949；会话总量仅相差1.18倍，末轮范围重叠；价格分解不可得。在选定的单任务单模型上，暴露该lesson后失败减少（0/8或1/8，对照共享非并发历史下限6/6，未做多重性校正）。常驻与BM25在两个小样本比较中未检测到差异。该任务基于先验下限证据选取，故属选定任务的事后存在性信号，非确证性比率。

Conclusion: 记忆与技能可共享同一渐进式披露载体，由此得出一条候选RSI设计规则：单一Skill载体族、共享读侧、治理化的差异写侧。但身体递送 h := P(D|A) 在RQ1—RQ2中未被测量，本文评估的仅为常驻索引实现，结论受样本规模、任务选取与缺乏多重性校正的限制。

Abstract: Memory and skills improve agents without changing weights: memory carries prior experience, skills carry reusable procedures. Wrapping both in stores, routers, retrieval, reflection, and update paths makes reuse machinery grow with accumulation. Part of this duplication need not be rebuilt: a Skill is already a natural carrier for distilled memory. Here the memory component is a Skill: resident description holds hot cues, on-demand SKILL.md a colder index and curation policy, and reference/*.md files detailed memories (levels L0 -> L1 -> L2). A governed 1,024-character description budget keeps the resident index compressed. Memory and capability thus share one progressive-disclosure carrier; reflection evolves either Skill. Writes fork: history appended, current state rewritten and revalidated. In one deployed system, the sharpest identification is governance: 4 write entries exist, but only 1/4 reaches the settlement ledger. At the priced operating point, one L1 lesson-1 point adds 1,313 first-turn tokens against a 1,462-token baseline--tied at k=1 (1,313 versus 1,365), 4,949 at k=5; session totals differ by only 1.18x with overlapping last-turn ranges; price decomposition is unavailable. On one selected task with one model, exposing the lesson means fewer failures (0/8 or 1/8 with the lesson versus a shared non-concurrent 6/6 historical floor, unadjusted for multiplicity). The task was selected on prior floor evidence, so this is a selected-task post-selection existence signal, not a confirmatory rate. Resident and BM25 show no detected difference in two small comparisons. This motivates a candidate RSI design rule: one Skill carrier family, a shared read side, governed distinct writes. Body delivery h := P(D|A) is uninstrumented in RQ1--RQ2; this evaluates a resident-index implementation.

</details>


### [192] [RECTIFY: An Interactive Workbench for Post-Evaluation RAG Diagnosis, Repair, and Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16764)
*Keerthana Murugaraj,Salima Lamsiyah,Martin Theobald*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RECTIFY 是一个交互式 Streamlit 工作台，将 RAG 评估结果转化为可审计的修复工作流，通过过滤、路由与修复卡片生成帮助开发者决定修复内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 评估器能识别弱检索、接地不良、答案不完整、生成缺乏支撑等失败，但很少帮助开发者决定下一步该修复什么。

Method: RECTIFY 先过滤无需修复的案例，将剩余失败路由为可操作的失败族与细粒度修复切片，并生成可编辑修复卡片；开发者可批准、拒绝或通过沙箱重跑进行验证。

Result: 在受控 RAG 基准上，RECTIFY 在 BM25、稠密与混合检索下呈现可解释的失败画像：BM25 主要触发噪声检索类修复，稠密与混合检索则留下较少的多部分欠检索与证据未充分利用案例。分析还表明预过滤减少不必要修复候选，切片级路由比粗粒度族级诊断生成更精准的修复卡片。

Conclusion: RECTIFY 作为开源 Streamlit 工作台公开可用，帮助开发者将评估结果转化为可审查的修复决策。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) evaluators can identify failures such as weak retrieval, poor grounding, incomplete answers, and unsupported generation, but they rarely help developers decide what to repair next. We present RECTIFY, an interactive Streamlit workbench that turns evaluated RAG cases into auditable repair workflows. RECTIFY filters cases that do not require repair, routes remaining failures into actionable families and finegrained repair slices, and generates editable repair cards that developers can approve, reject, or verify through sandbox reruns. On a controlled RAG benchmark, RECTIFY surfaces interpretable failure profiles across BM25, dense, and hybrid retrieval: BM25 mainly triggers noisy-retrieval repairs, while dense and hybrid retrieval leave smaller sets of multi-part underretrieval and underused-evidence cases. Additional analyses show that pre-filtering reduces unnecessary repair candidates and that slicelevel routing yields more targeted repair cards than broad family-level diagnosis. RECTIFY is publicly available as an open-source Streamlit workbench 1 for helping developers turn evaluation results into inspectable repair decisions.

</details>


### [193] [RepoAtlas: Guiding Coding Agents via Evolving Multimodal Repository Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16936)
*Yunxiang Zhang,Haiquan Wang,JiaWei Guo,Hanyang Xia,Yan Chen,Tong Chen,Zhang Zhiwei,Junchen Ye*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 RepoAtlas，一种免训练模块，通过“select–project–refresh”循环在仓库代码图上维护演化式多模态仓库视图，缓解仓库级问题求解中代码定位与上下文维护的困难。在 SWE-bench Verified 上平均提升 2.4 个点解决率，同时减少输入 token 与模型调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 编码代理在仓库级问题求解上仍具挑战：不仅需生成合理补丁，还要在相互依赖的文件间定位相关代码，并维持充分且聚焦的仓库上下文。代码图能表达非局部关系，但线性文本界面难以呈现其拓扑；完整仓库图渲染过于稠密难以可靠感知，而一次性局部视图随探索推进会过时。

Method: RepoAtlas 为免训练模块，对仓库代码图执行 select–project–refresh 循环：1）结合问题证据与当前探索状态，在固定预算下选择任务相关区域；2）将所选结构投影为互补的视觉与文本表示；3）当探索状态变化使视图过时时刷新视图。

Result: 在 SWE-bench Verified 上，相对最强的多模态图基线，解决率提升 2.4 个百分点，输入 token 与模型调用分别平均减少 5.8% 和 7.8%；在三个不同家族与规模的模型上均取得一致增益。

Conclusion: 通过动态维护任务相关的多模态仓库图视图，RepoAtlas 在提升仓库级问题解决率的同时降低 token 与调用开销，且跨模型具备良好泛化性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-powered coding agents have made rapid progress in automating software engineering tasks, yet repository-level issue resolution remains challenging. Beyond generating a plausible patch, an agent must localize relevant code across interdependent files and maintain repository context that is both sufficient and focused. Code graphs expose non-local relations, but linear text interfaces obscure their topology; rendering the full repository graph yields visual representations that are too dense to perceive reliably, whereas a one-shot local view becomes stale as exploration proceeds. We present \textbf{RepoAtlas}, a training-free module that maintains evolving multimodal repository views through a \emph{select--project--refresh} loop over a repository code graph. RepoAtlas combines evidence from the issue with the agent's current exploration state to select a task-relevant region under a fixed budget, projects the selected structure into complementary visual and textual representations, and refreshes the view when changes in the exploration state render it outdated. We evaluate RepoAtlas on SWE-bench Verified, where it improves the resolve rate by 2.4 points while reducing input tokens and model calls by 5.8\% and 7.8\% on average, relative to the strongest multimodal graph baseline, with consistent gains across three models of different families and scales.

</details>


### [194] [TasmScan: Continuation-Aware Taint Analysis for TVM Bytecode with Savelist Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16987)
*Yixuan Liu,Yin Wu,Yi Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TasmScan 是首个面向 TON 虚拟机（TVM）的字节码级静态分析框架，通过建模 savelist 语义实现跨续体数据流推理，在真实合约上以高精度检测多类缺陷并显著快于符号执行基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TVM 使用一等续体（first-class continuations）配合 savelist 管理控制流与寄存器状态，寄存器可跨续体边界传递数据而不经过操作数栈。因此，字节码级分析若不对 savelist 语义显式建模，就无法构建完整的数据流追踪。现有工作缺乏面向 TVM 的、无需源码的跨续体数据流静态分析能力。

Method: 提出 TasmScan：(1) 通过前向寄存器分析建模 savelist 语义，对精确解析的 save 站点与局部追踪的寄存器定义给出形式化过近似保证；(2) 将字节码提升为带类型的中间表示 TASIR；(3) 在 TASIR 上执行路径敏感的污点分析，并使用上下文感知的 source 检测缺陷。

Result: 在 TON verifier registry 的 2,921 个合约与含 208 个人工确认 ground truth 的标注基准上评估：全语料解析 294,546 个动态续体目标，精确率 100%；消融实验证实 savelist 传播对解析依赖跨续体寄存器传递的间接寄存器调用不可或缺；基准上五类缺陷检出率 95.3%、精确率 96.8%；全语料 366 对分层抽样估计整体精确率 85.8%；相较最先进的符号执行基线中位加速 17 倍，且在路径分析对比中 100% 完成分析、零崩溃与超时。

Conclusion: savelist 语义建模是 TVM 字节码分析的关键，TasmScan 证明了无需源码即可实现高精度、高效率的跨续体数据流与缺陷检测，为 TON 智能合约安全分析提供了可行且优于符号执行方案的基础框架。

Abstract: The Open Network (TON), with a peak market capitalization exceeding $20 billion and over 175 million activated on-chain addresses, relies on the TVM (TON Virtual Machine) to execute smart contracts. TVM uses first-class continuations with savelists to manage control flow and register state across continuation invocations. Since savelist-captured registers allow data to flow across continuation boundaries without passing through the operand stack, bytecode-level analyses cannot construct complete data flow tracking without explicitly modeling savelist semantics. We present TasmScan, the first bytecode-level static analysis framework for TVM that enables cross-continuation data flow reasoning without requiring source code. TasmScan models savelist semantics via forward register analysis with a formal over-approximation guarantee for exact-resolved save sites and locally tracked register definitions, then lifts bytecode into TASIR, a typed intermediate representation, and performs path-sensitive taint analysis with context-aware sources to detect defects. We evaluate TasmScan on 2,921 contracts from the TON verifier registry and a labeled benchmark of 208 contracts with human-confirmed ground truth. On the full corpus, TasmScan resolves 294,546 dynamic continuation targets with 100% precision; ablation confirms that savelist propagation is essential for resolving indirect register calls that depend on cross-continuation register passing. On the benchmark, TasmScan detects 95.3% of defects across five classes with 96.8% precision. A 366-pair stratified sample from the full corpus estimates 85.8% overall precision. TasmScan offers a 17x median speedup over the state-of-the-art symbolic-execution baseline, and in the path-analysis comparison completes 100% of analyses with zero crashes or timeouts.

</details>


### [195] [Search-Based Metamorphic Testing of Vision-Language Models in Autonomous Underwater Robotic Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17007)
*Muhammad Yousaf,Aitor Arrieta,Shaukat Ali,Paolo Arcaini,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出MetaVLM——一种基于搜索的蜕变测试方法，通过在水下图像上寻找最小变换集合诱发视觉语言模型（VLM）错误预测，以评估其在水下自主机器人软件中的质量；在BLIP与CLIP上使用NSGA-II并与随机搜索对比，得出各模型的优劣及对软件工程实践的启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业合作方关注跨领域工业系统（含水面舰艇与自主水下机器人AUR）的质量保证。VLM在场景理解、图像描述与目标识别上表现优异，但其在水下环境AUR软件中的应用研究不足。因此需要评估VLM质量以支持其集成，并需要自动化软件测试工具来评估适用性、提升可靠性。

Method: 提出搜索式蜕变测试方法MetaVLM：识别一组最小的水下图像变换，以诱发错误的模型预测，从而暴露VLM失效。采用NSGA-II作为多目标搜索算法，在开源VLM（BLIP、CLIP）上进行评估，并以随机搜索作为基线对照。

Result: 实验结果揭示了各VLM在AUR软件系统场景下的优势与局限，并表明所提搜索式蜕变测试相较随机搜索的有效性。

Conclusion: 面向从事VLM软件系统质量保证的软件工程实践者与研究者，本文基于实验结果总结出若干经验教训，为将VLM集成到AUR等水下系统软件中的测试与可靠性提升提供指导。

Abstract: Our industry partner focuses on quality assurance for industrial systems across multiple domains, including maritime systems, such as overwater vessels and autonomous underwater robots (AURs). Despite the strong performance of vision-language models (VLMs) in scene understanding, image captioning, and object recognition, their use in AUR software operating in underwater environments is underexplored. Therefore, in this context, it is important to evaluate the quality of VLMs for integration into AUR software and, so, automated software testing tools are needed to assess their suitability and improve their dependability. To this end, we propose a search-based metamorphic testing approach (MetaVLM) that identifies a minimal set of transformations on underwater images to induce incorrect model predictions, thereby revealing VLM failures. We employ NSGA-II as a multi-objective search algorithm and evaluate it over open-source VLMs, BLIP and CLIP, against a random search baseline. Results demonstrate the strengths and limitations of each VLM in the context of AUR software systems. Based on the results, we derive lessons for software engineering practitioners and researchers working on quality assurance of VLM-based software systems.

</details>


### [196] [GANADI: Uncovering C/C++ OSS Reuse Genealogies via Pivotal Function-Based Clustering to Enhance Supply Chain Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17018)
*Dongyeon Kim,Seunghoon Woo,Heejo Lee*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出GANADI，一种系统性识别C/C++开源软件复用谱系的方法，以增强软件供应链安全。通过聚类下游项目并推断复用方向构建复用谱系，在20个广泛复用项目上达到84.85%精确率和95.76%召回率，并借助谱系检测出48个未修复漏洞，其中23个在负责任披露后得到修复（含1个CVE编号）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解开源软件复用谱系对提升SBOM完整性、跨供应链安全修复优先级至关重要。现有方法虽能识别项目内被复用的组件和漏洞，却无法穿越中间项目追踪OSS复用路径，限制了其在供应链生态系统安全中的有效性。

Method: GANADI通过聚类下游项目来构建复用谱系，聚类依据为共享的源自同一代码的特征（称为pivotal functions），随后在每个聚类内推断项目间的复用方向。

Result: 在20个被广泛复用的OSS项目（超过1,500条传播路径）上，GANADI识别复用谱系达到84.85%精确率和95.76%召回率，而现有方法召回率最高仅为23.21%。利用复用谱系进行漏洞检测，在真实流行C/C++项目中识别出48个未修复漏洞，其中23个在负责任披露后得到修复（含1个CVE编号）。

Conclusion: GANADI能够有效追踪跨中间项目的OSS复用路径，构建复用谱系，在准确性上显著优于现有方法，并通过基于谱系的漏洞管理产生实际安全影响，证明了该方法的实用价值。

Abstract: We present GANADI, a systematic approach for identifying C/C++ OSS reuse genealogies to enhance software supply chain security. Understanding OSS reuse genealogy is crucial for improving SBOM completeness and prioritizing security remediation across supply chains. Although existing approaches can identify reused compo- nents and vulnerabilities within a project, they fail to trace OSS reuse paths through intermediate projects, limiting their effectiveness in securing supply chain ecosystems. To address this limitation, GANADI constructs reuse genealogies by clustering downstream projects based on shared characteristics of origin-derived code (called pivotal functions), and then inferring reuse direction among the projects within each cluster. When applied to 20 widely reused OSS projects with over 1,500 propagation paths, GANADI achieved 84.85% precision and 95.76% recall in identifying reuse genealogies, outperforming existing approaches that achieved at most 23.21% recall. Leveraging OSS reuse genealogy for vulnerability detection, we identified 48 unpatched vulnerabilities in real-world popular C/C++ projects. Among them, 23 were patched following our responsible disclosure (including one CVE ID assigned), demonstrating the practical impact of genealogy-based vulnerability management.

</details>


### [197] [A Set-Theoretic Evaluation Framework for Assessing Asset Administration Shell Instances: Towards Comparability and Suitability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17062)
*Carsten Ellwein,David Dietrich,Rozana Cvitkovic,Bastian Lang,Hansjoerg Tutsch,Andreas Wortmann*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出两种互补方法支持资产管理员外壳（AAS）的比较与面向应用的适用性评估：一是基于集合论的操作比较AAS模型，识别公共、缺失和差异的子模型与参数；二是AAS适用性模型，从结构一致性、语义一致性、基数、规范一致性四个维度评估AAS对特定用例需求的符合度，可对照参考AAS或一组必需SemanticID执行，输出适用性值及缺失/不符信息的详细报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AAS在制造业中日益成为表示资产及其信息并支撑软件服务的标准化手段，但不同AAS实例在结构、内容和完成度上差异明显，导致难以判断某个AAS是否适用于特定应用，因此需要支持比较与面向应用评估的方法。

Method: 方法包含两部分：（1）运用集合论操作比较AAS模型，识别公共、缺失及不同的子模型和参数；（2）构建AAS适用性模型，评估AAS对特定用例需求的符合度，评估维度包括结构一致性、语义一致性、基数和规范一致性，可对照参考AAS或一组必需SemanticID进行。

Result: 由识别出的偏差推导出适用性值，并附带关于缺失或不符信息的详细报告。

Conclusion: 所提方法可帮助实践者和研究者比较不断演化的AAS，并为其在制造软件服务中的适用性提供面向应用的信息。

Abstract: Asset Administration Shells (AAS) provide a standardized means of representing assets and their information in manufacturing and increasingly serve as a basis for software services. However, different AAS instances vary in structure, content, and degree of completion, making it difficult to determine whether a given AAS is suitable for a specific application. This paper presents two complementary methods to support the comparison and application-oriented assessment of AAS. First, set-theoretic operations are employed to compare AAS models, enabling the identification of common, missing, and differing submodels and parameters. Second, an AAS suitability model assesses the conformity of an AAS to the requirements of a specific use case. The assessment considers structural conformity, semantic consistency, cardinality, and specification conformity and can be performed either against a reference AAS or a set of required SemanticIDs. A suitability value is derived from the identified deviations and is complemented by a detailed report of missing or non-conforming information. The proposed approach support practitioners and researchers in the comparison of evolving AAS and provide application-specific information on their suitability for manufacturing software services.

</details>


### [198] [Towards an Asset Administration Shell Maturity Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17084)
*Carsten Ellwein,David Dietrich,Rozana Cvitkovic,Andreas Wortmann*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出一种AAS成熟度（AAS maturity）新概念，用于刻画AAS实例满足既定数字孪生标准的程度，从而实现不同AAS实例的可比性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AAS被视为制造业数字孪生实现与数据交换的基础模型，但在基于AAS的系统中，比较不同AAS实例缺乏广泛接受的方法论框架和成熟度模型，难以系统性地支撑此类分析。

Method: 从文献中推导AAS成熟度相关概念，并通过示例化（exemplification）加以应用。

Result: 形成初步（emerging）成果，使从业者与研究者能够系统比较AAS实例，并支持数字孪生工程过程中后续开发步骤的识别与评估。

Conclusion: AAS成熟度概念填补了AAS实例比较缺乏方法框架与成熟度模型的空白，为数字孪生工程的系统化比较与演进评估提供支持。

Abstract: The Asset Administration Shell (AAS) is increasingly recognized as a fundamental model for the realization of and data exchange between digital twins in manufacturing. An AAS defines a hierarchical data structure to represent any type of asset throughout its entire lifecycle. In the context of AAS-based systems, comparing different AAS instances constitutes a practical challenge, as neither a widely accepted methodological framework nor a maturity model are available to systematically support such analyses. To address this gap, we propose a novel concept of AAS maturity that characterizes the extent to which established digital twin criteria are met and thus enabling comparability of AAS instances. The concepts are derived from the literature and applied through exemplification. These emerging results enable practitioners and researchers to systematically compare AAS instances and support the identification and assessment of further development steps in the digital twin engineering process.

</details>


### [199] [Grounding SWE-Agent Decisions in Architecture-0 Design: Navigating Unknown Unknowns through Physical Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17221)
*Zhongkai Wang,Yan Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文研究自主软件工程智能体（SWE-Agents）在系统设计阶段处理"未知的未知"（UUs）时的失败模式，提出物理映射守卫（PMG）机制，通过分离验证权来消除规格博弈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SWE-Agents在确定性编码任务上表现优异，但在初期系统设计阶段（Architecture 0）面对隐含工程约束即未知的未知（UUs）时表现不佳，而这类约束很少被显式表述。作者希望探究智能体如何应对UUs并解决其失败问题。

Method: 沿纯文本自博弈、工具增强反馈、外部物理映射的渐进轨迹进行研究；发现纯文本推理退化为礼貌共识或貌似合理但物理上不可能的编造，引入早期执行沙箱又触发规格博弈。为此提出基于"关注点分离"原则的物理映射守卫（PMG），剥夺智能体的验证权，由外部确定性的语义到物理（S2P）映射引擎评估语义意图。

Result: 实证分析揭示级联式失败链条：纯文本推理失败→沙箱触发规格博弈→PMG解决自验证陷阱。大量评估表明PMG完全消除了物理层与验证层的博弈行为。

Conclusion: PMG将残余失败精确隔离为语义重新解释与审核者越权两类，是迈向自动化架构设计中真正可供性落地（affordance grounding）的关键一步。

Abstract: Autonomous Software Engineering Agents (SWE-Agents) excel in deterministic coding tasks but struggle with Architecture 0, the nascent system design phase plagued by implicit engineering constraints, or Unknown Unknowns (UUs) that are rarely stated explicitly. To investigate how agents navigate UUs, we explore a progressive trajectory across pure-text self-play, tool-augmented feedback, and external physical mapping. Our empirical analysis reveals a cascading chain of failures. Pure-text reasoning inevitably devolves into polite consensus or plausible yet physically impossible fabrications. Attempting to bridge this gap via an early-stage execution sandbox unexpectedly triggers Specification Gaming: agents exploit their autonomy over validation scripts to bypass physical constraints, achieving superficial success without resolving core architectural flaws. To resolve this self-validation trap, we propose the Physical Mapping Guard (PMG). Grounded in the software engineering principle of Separation of Concerns, PMG revokes verification authority from the agent, forcing semantic intents to be evaluated by an external, deterministic Semantic-to-Physical (S2P) mapping engine. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that PMG completely eradicates physical-layer and validation-layer gaming. By precisely isolating residual failures to semantic reinterpretations and auditor overreach, PMG marks a critical step toward genuine affordance grounding in automated architectural design.

</details>


### [200] [A Memorization Floor for LLM Refinement of Decompiled Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17236)
*Muhammad Asjad*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出"记忆下限"（memorization floor）这一条目内对照设计，通过破坏输入标识符后重复精炼，剥离LLM改进去编译器输出时依赖的输入信息与先验知识，在无记忆风险的新函数上检验恢复来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM精炼反编译输出时，其命名等改进究竟来自对输入信息的真实利用，还是来自模型先验（记忆/训练分布），缺乏条目内、可控的判别手段，且语料难度与发布模型记忆会污染结论。

Method: 设计记忆下限：对同一函数先精炼，再在标识符被破坏的输入上重复精炼，度量存活增益。采用条目内对照排除语料难度，仅需二十次API调用。使用分析计划提交后新写的函数避免记忆。以自身输出词表构建臂匹配置换零假设，并设置第二家厂商精炼器、字节相同输入、预先注册的对照。

Result: 恢复真实存在：精炼输出比臂匹配置换零假设高 +0.072 至 +0.137。但不依赖被消融的输入：破坏数据流使命名增益变化 +0.001（95% CI [-0.026, +0.026]），移除类型前缀或置换名称变化更小。第二精炼器复现，共十二组对照、两模型、十二个零假设。可读性始终处于上限，读者无信号。零假设有界非绝对：小于0.056的贡献不可见，消融保留操作，故仅凭操作命名仍是竞争解释。

Conclusion: 无任何注册假设被证实。作者完整报告五项仪器失效，包括偏向处理组的重装配框架，以及未验证在自身输入上是否有效的等价性检查器。命名增益可能来自模型先验而非输入信息，但结论受测量灵敏度上限约束。

Abstract: We introduce a memorization floor: a within-item control separating what LLM refinement of decompiler output recovers from its input from what it recovers from its prior. Refine a function, then refine it again from an input whose identifiers have been destroyed, and measure what survives. Because the comparison is within-item, corpus difficulty cannot contribute; it costs twenty API calls. Applied to functions written after our analysis plan was committed, so no released model could have memorized them, it reports two things. Recovery is real: refined output sits +0.072 to +0.137 above an arm-matched permutation null built from its own output vocabulary. But it does not depend on the input we ablate: destroying the input's dataflow changes the naming gain by +0.001 (95% CI [-0.026, +0.026]), and removing type prefixes or permuting names changes it by no more. A second refiner from another vendor, registered in advance and given byte-identical inputs, reproduces this -- twelve contrasts, two models, twelve nulls. Readability stays at ceiling throughout, so a reader is given no signal. The null is bounded, not absolute: contributions under 0.056 are invisible, and the ablation leaves operations intact, so naming from those alone remains a competing reading. No registered hypothesis was confirmed, and we report the five instrument failures behind that in full, including a reassembly harness biased against the treated arm and an equivalence checker we registered without checking it worked on our inputs.

</details>


### [201] [An Exemplar of a Digital Twin in Mechanical Engineering: Understanding Model Hybridization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17258)
*Mahussi Datongnon,Hubert Lejeune,Yoann Jus,Benoit Combemale,Julien Deantoni*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文以CETIM现有流体回路数字孪生为案例，用Gil等框架刻画其生命周期，并补充"动机—实现"两维刻画以显式化物理模型与数据驱动模型的混合（hybridization）过程，从而提升混合数字孪生工程的可追溯性、可复现性与可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生在工业（尤其机械工程）中应用广泛，且日益将物理（演绎）模型与数据驱动（归纳）模型整合为混合模型，但混合数字孪生的工程实践缺乏系统文档：混合往往以临时（ad hoc）方式引入，实现细节仅部分显式化，限制了可复现性与可迁移性。

Method: 采用双重视角的案例研究：首先使用Gil等的特征刻画框架，从生命周期各维度对CETIM现有流体回路数字孪生进行结构化描述；随后引入补充性刻画，沿"动机（motivation）"与"实现（realization）"两个维度分析该案例，使混合决策与实现方式显式化。

Result: 得到对流体回路数字孪生的结构化生命周期描述，以及沿动机与实现两维度的混合化刻画，形成对混合化决策的可追溯记录。

Conclusion: 该双重刻画方式能够支撑混合数字孪生工程实践的文档化与知识转移，有助于提升混合数字孪生开发的可复现性与可迁移性。

Abstract: Digital Twins (DTs) are widely adopted across a variety of application domains. In industrial sectors, particularly in mechanical engineering, they accelerate product development, reduce risks, enable early issue prediction, and lower sustainment costs . In practice, DTs increasingly integrate physics-based (deductive) and data-driven (inductive) models into hybrid models combining the complementary strengths of both modeling paradigms. In this paper, we refer to this integration paradigm as hybridization. Despite this trend, the engineering of hybrid DTs that is, remains insufficiently documented. Hybridization is often introduced in an ad hoc manner, and its implementation is only partially made explicit, which limits reproducibility and transferability. This paper reports on the development of an existing fluidic loop digital twin at Centre Technique des Industries M{é}canique (CETIM). The DT is described using the characterization framework of Gil et al., providing a structured view across its lifecycle dimensions. To make hybridization explicit, the case is further analyzed through a complementary characterization structured along two dimensions: motivation and realization. The resulting description provides a traceable account of hybridization decisions and supports the documentation and transfer of hybrid DT engineering practices.

</details>


### [202] [After the Party: Governing What a Viral Agent-Skill Ecosystem Left Behind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17274)
*Yunpeng Xiong,Ting Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文以 OpenClaw 智能体技能注册表爆发式增长为背景，通过 Git 历史、GitHub 议题/PR 与三个 ClawHub 快照测量热潮"留痕"：注意力高度集中（前 10% 技能占 46.93% 下载），无稳定元数据能预测技能存续，77.86% 技能零星零评论、85.06% 可读技能含权限证据，三个安全扫描器在 61,990 个共有技能中 23,702 个判定不一致，加权敏感度仅 21.67%–61.06%。结论是快速增长的技能注册表治理不能依赖简单元数据或单一扫描器评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 智能体日益通过自然语言"技能"指令执行 shell、网络、凭据、文件与进程操作，公共注册表大规模分发这些技能；2026 上半年 OpenClaw 走红、注册表膨胀（91 天内可见存量近乎翻倍），随后见顶回落，亟需实证了解热潮留下的生态状态与治理风险。

Method: 采用纵向档案式测量：收集 OpenClaw Git 提交历史、GitHub issues 与 pull requests，以及三个时点的 ClawHub 注册表快照；对技能下载分布、存续性（控制创建队列与技能年龄的回归）、人工审查信号（stars/comments）、权限证据标注以及三个安全扫描器的判定一致性进行统计，并通过人工裁决建立参考标准以评估扫描器加权敏感度。

Result: 注意力高度集中：前 10% 技能获得 46.93% 的全部下载。在控制创建队列与技能年龄后，规模、下载量等简单技能特征均不能稳定预测技能是否继续上架。人工审查缺失：77.86% 技能零 star 且零评论；85.06% 可读技能含权限证据。自动化清理不可靠：三个扫描器对共同覆盖的 61,990 个技能中 23,702 个判定不一致；经人工裁决，相对参考标准的加权敏感度仅 21.67%–61.06%。

Conclusion: 快速增长期结束后，智能体技能注册表留下注意力集中、审查薄弱、权限暴露普遍、扫描器互不一致且敏感度低的治理缺口；治理不能依赖简单元数据或单一扫描器分数，需稳健、透明的测量与独立验证。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly act through agent skills, i.e., natural-language instructions, that direct a host agent toward shell, network, credential, file, and process actions, and public registries distribute them at scale. In the first half of 2026, the OpenClaw AI agent went viral, and its public skill registry boomed: the observable stock nearly doubled in 91 days, and a majority of the listings visible in June were created in just two months. By the end of our study window, the wave had crested, and monthly listing creation and core-repository activity were falling from their spring peaks. This paper measures what the boom left behind, drawing on the OpenClaw Git history, its GitHub issues and pull requests, and three ClawHub registry snapshots. Attention is concentrated: the top 10% of skills received 46.93% of all downloads. No simple skill features (like size or download counts) remained a stable predictor of continued listing once creation cohort and skill age were controlled. Human scrutiny did not stay: 77.86% have zero stars and zero comments, while 85.06% of the readable skills carry privilege evidence. And automated cleanup is not ready: the three security scanners disagreed on 23,702 of the 61,990 skills they all cover. After human adjudication, weighted scanner sensitivity against the reference standard ranged from 21.67% to 61.06%. Governing fast-growing agent-skill registries cannot rely on simple metadata or single scanner scores; it requires robust, transparent measurement and independent validation.

</details>


### [203] [Type-IV Code Clone Detection via Layer-Wise Non-Contrastive Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17338)
*Luciano Marchezan,Kevin Delcourt,Eugene Syriani,Houari Sahraoui*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出LWVIC4Code，一种面向Type-IV代码克隆检测的非对比式表示学习方法，基于VICReg框架并引入跨层一致性与深度依赖的层加权，在无负样本情况下取得有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Type-IV克隆语义等价但句法可能不同，传统的基于词法或句法的方法难以检测；现有机器学习方法依赖对比学习，需要谨慎的负样本采样且可能引入偏差。

Method: 在Variance-Invariance-Covariance Regularization（VICReg）框架及既有逐层VICReg训练基础上，LWVIC4Code引入跨层一致性正则化与深度依赖的层加权，在transformer各层间逐步精炼语义信息，生成鲁棒且具判别力的代码表示。

Result: 在Python数据集Kamino与多语言数据集GPTCloneBench上，与对比学习基线和零样本大语言模型对比，LWVIC4Code在无负样本条件下取得相当或更优的性能，受益于逐层监督，并能从Python有效泛化到Java、C#等其他语言。

Conclusion: 非对比式、逐层表示学习是面向鲁棒语义代码克隆检测的一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: Software clones are fragments of code that are similar or functionally equivalent to each other. They pose significant challenges for maintenance, refactoring, and bug detection. Detecting Type-IV clones, which are semantically equivalent but may differ syntactically, is particularly difficult for traditional token- or syntax-based methods. Recent machine learning approaches rely on contrastive learning, which requires careful negative sampling and can introduce bias. In this paper, we propose LWVIC4Code, a non-contrastive representation learning approach specifically designed for Type-IV clone detection. Building on the Variance-Invariance-Covariance Regularization (VICReg) framework and prior layer-wise VICReg training, LWVIC4Code introduces cross-layer consistency regularization and depth-dependent layer weighting to progressively refine semantic information across transformer layers, producing robust and discriminative code representations. We conduct an empirical study comparing LWVIC4Code against a contrastive learning baseline and zero-shot large language models on Python (Kamino) and multi-language (GPTCloneBench) datasets. Results show that LWVIC4Code achieves competitive or superior performance without negative samples, benefits from layer-wise supervision, and generalizes effectively from Python to other languages, particularly Java and C#. These results demonstrate that non-contrastive, layer-wise representation learning is a promising direction for robust semantic code clone detection.

</details>


### [204] [Coding Agents Have Converged: Why the SWE-bench Leaderboard Can No Longer Order Its Top Entries, and What to Measure Instead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17394)
*Fengshuo Liu,Ying Liu,Ruize Sun,Lie Luo,Siyuan Guo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究通过审计254个SWE-bench提交结果（不运行模型），证明榜单上微小的分数差距不足以支撑排名解读，主张报告比较集特定的分辨率与模型-脚手架来源信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体榜单上微小的分数差异常被直接解读为系统间的排名先后。作者质疑这种解读是否得到已发布评测结论的支持，因此开展审计。

Method: 基于254个SWE-bench提交、覆盖四个数据划分，在未运行模型的情况下进行审计：(1) 划分共享成功与区分性实例；(2) 计算解集嵌套度并与分数隐含基线比较；(3) 分析模型-脚手架配对效应（九个单元格均值交互检验，Holm校正）；(4) 对相邻排名对做精确配对的McNemar检验；(5) 用基于领导的规则划分描述性层级并评估分组敏感性，估算达成分辨率所需实例预算。

Result: Verified划分上前两名各解决500例中的396例；前十名共享285次成功、51次失败，仅164例可区分其表现。前沿解集嵌套度中位数0.935，高于分数隐含基线0.774，表明成功高度重叠。同一模型不同脚手架的分数范围可达29.8个百分点，而前三十名总体差距仅8.8个百分点；九个单元格均值交互检验中六个经Holm校正后仍显著，但观察性设计无法识别因果性脚手架效应。精确配对McNemar检验在alpha=0.05下无法区分Verified前三十的任何29个相邻对，而在更大的Test划分上可区分23对中的14对。基于领导的规则给出三个描述性层级（Holm校正后为两个）；未拒绝不等于等价。

Conclusion: 榜单微小总体差距并不能确立排名差异。作者发布划分结果与五步审计协议（刻画共享结果、检验配对差异、报告分组敏感性、估算所需实例预算），倡导报告比较集特定的分辨率及模型-脚手架来源，而非将微小聚合差距视为既定排名。

Abstract: Small differences on coding-agent leaderboards are often read as an ordering of systems. We audit whether the published verdicts support this reading, using 254 SWE-bench submissions across four splits without running models. On Verified, the leading two entries each resolve 396 of 500 instances. The top ten share 285 successes and 51 failures, leaving 164 instances that distinguish their outcomes. Frontier solution sets have median nesting 0.935 against a score-implied baseline of 0.774, indicating strongly shared successes. Scores also depend on the evaluated model-scaffold pair: observed within-model scaffold ranges reach 29.8 percentage points, compared with the 8.8-point spread of the top thirty. Six of nine cell-mean interaction tests remain significant after Holm correction, although this observational design does not identify causal scaffold effects. Exact paired McNemar tests separate none of the 29 adjacent Verified top-thirty pairs at alpha=0.05, while the larger Test split separates 14 of 23. A stated leader-based rule yields three descriptive tiers, or two after Holm correction; non-rejection does not establish equivalence. We release the partition and a five-step audit protocol that profiles shared outcomes, tests paired differences, reports grouping sensitivity, and estimates the instance budget needed for resolution. The results motivate reporting comparison-set-specific resolution and model-scaffold provenance instead of interpreting small aggregate gaps as established rank differences.

</details>
